──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n46\t\n47\t/// Global pointer to the shutdown flag, used by synchronous signal handlers.\n48\t/// This is necessary because signal handlers run in a separate context and\n49\t/// cannot easily access the Worker's shutdown flag directly.\n50\t#[cfg(unix)]\n51\tstatic GLOBAL_SHUTDOWN_FLAG: AtomicUsize = AtomicUsize::new(0);\n52\t\n53\t/// Set the global shutdown flag pointer.\n54\t/// Called by `install_signal_handlers` to register the shutdown flag.\n55\t#[cfg(unix)]\n56\tfn set_global_shutdown_flag(ptr: usize) {\n57\t    GLOBAL_SHUTDOWN_FLAG.store(ptr, AtomicOrdering::SeqCst);\n58\t}\n59\t\n60\t/// Clear the global shutdown flag pointer.\n61\t/// Called when the worker is dropped to avoid dangling pointers.\n62\t#[cfg(unix)]\n63\tfn clear_global_shutdown_flag() {\n64\t    GLOBAL_SHUTDOWN_FLAG.store(0, AtomicOrdering::SeqCst);\n65\t    LAST_SIGNAL.store(0, AtomicOrdering::SeqCst);\n66\t}\n67\t\n68\t/// Track the last received signal for diagnostic logging.\n69\t/// AtomicU32 allows lock-free reads/writes from the signal handler.\n70\t#[cfg(unix)]\n71\tstatic LAST_SIGNAL: AtomicU32 = AtomicU32::new(0);\n72\t\n73\t/// Synchronous signal handler for SIGTERM, SIGINT, and SIGHUP.\n74\t///\n75\t/// This function is called directly by the OS when a signal is received.\n76\t/// It must be async-signal-safe: no allocation, no locking, no I/O.\n77\t/// We set the atomic shutdown flag, record the signal number, and return immediately.\n78\t#[cfg(unix)]\n79\textern \"C\" fn signal_handler(sig: i32) {\n80\t    // SAFETY: The signal handler is only installed after set_global_shutdown_flag\n81\t    // has been called with a valid pointer. The pointer remains valid for the\n82\t    // entire lifetime of the worker process.\n83\t    let ptr = GLOBAL_SHUTDOWN_FLAG.load(AtomicOrdering::SeqCst) as *const AtomicBool;\n84\t    if !ptr.is_null() {\n85\t        // SAFETY: The pointer is valid and points to an AtomicBool that lives\n86\t        // for the entire program duration.\n87\t        unsafe {\n88\t            (*ptr).store(true, AtomicOrdering::SeqCst);\n89\t        }\n90\t        // Record the signal number so the main loop can log it.\n91\t        LAST_SIGNAL.store(sig as u32, AtomicOrdering::SeqCst);\n92\t    }\n93\t}\n94\t\n95\t/// Install synchronous signal handlers for SIGTERM, SIGINT, and SIGHUP.\n96\t///\n97\t/// Uses libc::sigaction to register handlers that set the shutdown flag\n98\t/// immediately when a signal is received. This ensures that signals are\n99\t/// caught even if the tokio runtime hasn't polled async signal tasks yet.\n100\t#[cfg(unix)]\n101\tunsafe fn install_unix_signal_handlers() {\n102\t    use libc::{sigaction, sigemptyset, SA_RESTART, SIGHUP, SIGINT, SIGTERM};\n103\t\n104\t    // Set up the sigaction structure.\n105\t    let mut act: libc::sigaction = std::mem::zeroed();\n106\t    act.sa_sigaction = signal_handler as *const () as usize;\n107\t    // Block all signals during handler execution to prevent re-entrancy issues.\n108\t    sigemptyset(&mut act.sa_mask as *mut libc::sigset_t);\n109\t    // Use SA_RESTART to automatically restart system calls interrupted by signals.\n110\t    act.sa_flags = SA_RESTART;\n111\t\n112\t    // Install handlers for SIGTERM, SIGINT, and SIGHUP.\n113\t    // We ignore errors here - if a handler can't be installed, we'll log a\n114\t    // warning but continue. The async handlers (below) provide a fallback.\n115\t    for &sig in &[SIGTERM, SIGINT, SIGHUP] {\n116\t        let mut old: libc::sigaction = std::mem::zeroed();\n117\t        if sigaction(sig, &act, &mut old) == 0 {\n118\t            tracing::debug!(signal = sig, \"installed synchronous signal handler\");\n119\t        } else {\n120\t            // Log the error but don't fail - the async handlers provide a fallback.\n121\t            tracing::warn!(\n122\t                signal = sig,\n123\t                errno = *libc::__errno_location(),\n124\t                \"failed to install synchronous signal handler\"\n125\t            );\n126\t        }\n127\t    }\n128\t}\n129\t\n130\t/// Global state for atexit handler to emit worker.stopped on unexpected termination.\n131\t///\n132\t/// When a worker is killed externally (e.g., SIGKILL from capacity governor),\n133\t/// the normal signal handlers don't run. The atexit handler provides a last-resort\n134\t/// mechanism to emit worker.stopped telemetry with diagnostic information.\n135\tstatic ATEXIT_WORKER_STATE: Mutex<Option<AtexitWorkerState>> = `install_signal_handlers` to ensure the atexit handler can\n149\t/// emit meaningful telemetry if the process terminates unexpectedly.\n150\tfn register_atexit_handler(\n151\t    worker_name:        worker_name,\n159\t        beads_processed,\n160\t        start_time,\n161\t        last_state,\n162\t        log_file_path,\n163\t    };\n164\t    *ATEXIT_WORKER_STATE.lock().unwrap() = Some(state);\n165\t\n166\t    // Register the atexit handler.\n167\t    // This will run when the process exits normally, but NOT on SIGKILL.\n168\t    extern \"C\" fn atexit_handler() {\n169\t        if let Some(state) = ATEXIT_WORKER_STATE.lock().unwrap().as_ref() {\n170\t            let uptime = state.start_time.elapsed().as_secs();\n171\t            // Try to write to stderr as a last resort since telemetry may be unavailable.\n172\t            eprintln!(\n173\t                \"NEEDLE worker '{}' stopped unexpectedly: state={}, beads_processed={}, uptime={}s\",\n174\t                state.worker_name, state.last_state, state.beads_processed, uptime\n175\t            );\n176\t            eprintln!(\"This indicates the worker was killed by an external process (e.g., SIGKILL, OOM, capacity governor)\");\n177\t\n178\t            // Try to write a worker.stopped event to the JSONL log file.\n179\t            // This provides diagnostic information even when the worker is killed abruptly.\n180\t            if let Some(ref log_path) = state.log_file_path {\n181\t                use std::fs::OpenOptions;\n182\t                use std::io::Write;\n183\t\n184\t                let \"via_atexit_handler\": true\n194\t                    }\n195\t                });\n196\t\n197\t                if let Ok(mut file) = OpenOptions::new().create(true).append(true).open(log_path) {\n198\t                    let _ = writeln!(file, \"{}\", event);\n199\t                    let _ = file.flush();\n200\t                }\n201\t            }\n202\t        }\n203\t    }\n204\t\n205\t platforms\n246\t}\n247\t\n248\t/// TTL for race-lost bead exclusions.\n249\t///\n250\t/// After losing a claim race, a bead is excluded from selection for this duration\n251\t/// to prevent infinite loops where the selector returns the same bead repeatedly.\n252\tconst RACE_LOST_EXCLUSION_TTL: Duration = Duration::from_secs(30);\n253\t\n254\t/// Timeout for HANDLING state watchdog.\n255\t///\n256\t/// If the worker remains in HANDLING state for longer than this duration,\n257\t/// the watchdog thread will force a recovery. This is longer than the\n258\t/// inner timeouts (50s, 60s, 90s) to allow normal recovery to work first.\n259\tconst HANDLING_WATCHDOG_TIMEOUT_SECS: u64 = 120;\n260\t\n261\t/// The NEEDLE worker — owns and drives the full state machine.\n262\tpub struct Worker {\n263\t    config: Config,\n264\t    worker_name: String,\n265\t    store: Arc<dyn BeadStore>,\n266\t    /// Home workspace store — kept for restore after processing a remote bead.\n267\t    home_store: Arc<dyn BeadStore>,\n268\t    telemetry: Telemetry,\n269\t    strands: StrandRunner,\n270\t    claimer: Claimer,\n271\t    prompt_builder: PromptBuilder,\n272\t    dispatcher: Dispatcher,\n273\t    outcome_handler: OutcomeHandler,\n274\t    health: HealthMonitor,\n275\t    registry: Registry,\n276\t    rate_limiter: RateLimiter,\n277\t    mitosis_evaluator: MitosisEvaluator,\n278\t\n279\t    // State machine fields\n280\t    state: WorkerState,\n281\t    current_bead: Option<Bead>,\n282\t    exclusion_set: HashSet<BeadId>,\n283\t    /// Race-lost exclusions with TTL — prevents re-selecting beads that just lost a claim race.\n284\t    /// Each entry is (bead_id, expiration_time). Entries are pruned on access.\n285\t    race_lost_exclusions: Vec<(BeadId, Instant)>,\n286\t    /// Beads that lost a claim race in the current selection cycle.\n287\t    /// These are added to exclusion_set to prevent immediate re-selection.\n288\t    /// Cleared at the start of the next SELECTING cycle.\n289\t    race_lost_this_cycle: HashSet<BeadId>,\n290\t    retry_count: u32,\n291\t    consecutive_race_lost: u32,\n292\t    beads_processed: u64,\n293\t    shutdown: Arc<AtomicBool>,\n294\t    last_error: Option<anyhow::Error>,\n295\t    boot_time: Option<Instant>,\n296\t\n297\t    // Transient fields — pass data between state handlers within a single cycle.\n298\t    built_prompt: Option<BuiltPrompt>,\n299\t    current_strand: Option<String>,\n300\t    exec_output: Option<(AgentOutcome, bool)>,\n301\t    /// Effort tracking data for the current bead cycle.\n302\t    last_effort: Option<EffortData>,\n303\t    /// HEAD SHA captured just before agent dispatch; used to detect new commits.\n304\t    pre_dispatch_head: Option<String>,\n305\t    /// The workspace of the current bead store — updated when switching to remote.\n306\t    /// Used to ensure heartbeat reports the actual workspace where work is happening.\n307\t    current_workspace: PathBuf,\n308\t    /// Whether `worker.booting` was already emitted externally (e.g., from CLI layer).\n309\t    /// When true, `run()` skips emitting the booting event to avoid duplicates.\n310\t    booting_emitted: bool,\n311\t    /// Waterfall restart count from the most recent select cycle (for exhausted telemetry).\n312\t    last_waterfall_restarts: u32,\n313\t    /// Names of strands that triggered waterfall restarts in the most recent cycle.\n314\t    last_restart_triggers: Vec<String>,\n315\t    /// Strand evaluations from the most recent select cycle (for exhausted telemetry).\n316\t    last_strand_evaluations: Vec<(String, String, u64)>,\n317\t    /// Timestamp when the worker entered HANDLING state.\n318\t    /// Used by the watchdog to detect stuck HANDLING state.\n319\t    handling_state_entered_at: Option<Instant>,\n320\t    /// Flag set by the watchdog thread when HANDLING state timeout is detected.\n321\t    /// The main worker loop checks this flag and forces recovery if set.\n322\t    watchdog_triggered: Arc<AtomicBool>,\n323\t    /// Handle to the watchdog thread for cleanup on worker drop.\n324\t    #[allow(dead_code)]\n325\t    watchdog_handle: Option<std::thread::JoinHandle<()>>,\n326\t    /// The current bead lifecycle span guard. Created when a bead is claimed,\n327\t    /// dropped when the bead lifecycle ends (after HANDLING or when the bead is released).\n328\t    #[allow(dead_code)]\n329\t    bead_lifecycle_span: Option<tracing::span::EnteredSpan>,\n330\t    /// The last outcome for the current bead (used to record on bead.lifecycle span).\n331\t    last_outcome: Option<String>,\n332\t}\n333\t\n334\timpl Worker {\n335\t    /// Construct a worker using a pre-existing telemetry instance.\n336\t    ///\n337\t    /// Use this when telemetry has already been started (e.g. after emitting\n338\t    /// `worker.booting` from the CLI layer) so that early init steps are\n339\t    /// visible in the JSONL log.\n340\t    pub fn new_with_telemetry(\n341\t        config: Config,\n342\t        worker_name: String,\n343\t        store: Arc<dyn BeadStore>,\n344\t        telemetry: Telemetry,\n345\t    ) -> Self {\n346\t        Self::build(config, worker_name, store, telemetry, true)\n347\t    }\n348\t\n349\t    /// Construct a worker from config, a worker name, and a bead store implementation.\n350\t    ///\n351\t    /// Creates its own telemetry instance. falling back\");\n359\t                Telemetry::new(qualified_id.clone())\n360\t            });\n361\t        Self::build(config, worker_name, store, telemetry, false)\n362\t    }\n363\t\n364\t    /// Shared construction logic used by both [`new`] and [`new_with_telemetry`].\n365\t    fn build(\n366\t        config: Config,\n367\t        worker_name: String,\n368\t        store: Arc<dyn config.worker.max_claim_retries,\n380\t            100,\n381\t            telemetry.clone(),\n382\t        );\n383\t        let prompt_builder = PromptBuilder::with_workspace(\n384\t            &config.prompt,\n385\t            &config.workspace.default,\n386\t        )\n387\t        .unwrap_or_else(|e| {\n388\t            tracing::warn!(error = %e, \"failed to load workspace learnings, &config.strands.explore.workspaces,\n393\t            &config.workspace.labels,\n394\t        )\n395\t        .with_global_learnings(&config.strands.learning.global_learnings_file);\n396\t        let using built-in defaults\");\n400\t                let builtins = crate::dispatch::builtin_adapters()\n401\t                    .into_iter()\n402\t                    .map(|a| (a.name.clone(), a))\n403\t                    .collect();\n404\t                Dispatcher::with_adapters(builtins, telemetry.clone(), config.agent.timeout)\n405\t            }\n406\t        };\n407\t        let gracefully stop with worker.stopped telemetry.\n413\t        let shutdown = Arc::new(AtomicBool::new(false));\n414\t\n415\t        let health = HealthMonitor::new(\n416\t            config.clone(),\n417\t            worker_name.clone(),\n418\t            telemetry.clone(),\n419\t            Some(shutdown.clone()),\n420\t        );\n421\t        let registry = Registry::default_location(&config.workspace.home);\n422\t        let rate_limiter =\n423\t            RateLimiter::new(config.limits.clone(), &config.workspace.home.join(\"state\"));\n424\t        let mitosis_evaluator = MitosisEvaluator::new(\n425\t            config.strands.mitosis.clone(),\n426\t            telemetry.clone(),\n427\t            std::path::PathBuf::from(\"/tmp\"),\n428\t        );\n429\t\n430\t        // Restore beads_processed from registry if this worker was previously registered\n431\t        // (e.g., hot-reload resume). New workers start at 0.\n432\t        // Match by qualified identity ({adapter}-{worker_id}).\n433\t        let qualified_id = format!(\"{}-{}\", config.agent.default, worker_name);\n434\t        let beads_processed = registry\n435\t            .list()\n436\t            .ok()\n437\t            .and_then(|workers| workers.into_iter().find(|w| w.id == qualified_id))\n438\t            .map(|entry| entry.beads_processed)\n439\t            .unwrap_or(0);\n440\t\n441\t        let default_workspace = config.workspace.default.clone();\n442\t\n443\t        // Create the watchdog trigger flag before creating the Worker.\n444\t        let watchdog_triggered = Arc::new(AtomicBool::new(false));\n445\t\n446\t        Worker {\n447\t            config,\n448\t            worker_name,\n449\t            home_store: store.clone(),\n450\t            store,\n451\t            telemetry,\n452\t            strands,\n453\t            claimer,\n454\t            prompt_builder,\n455\t            dispatcher,\n456\t            outcome_handler,\n457\t            health,\n458\t            registry,\n459\t            rate_limiter,\n460\t            mitosis_evaluator,\n461\t            state: WorkerState::Booting,\n462\t            current_bead: None,\n463\t            exclusion_set: HashSet::new(),\n464\t            race_lost_exclusions: Vec::new(),\n465\t            race_lost_this_cycle: HashSet::new(),\n466\t            retry_count: 0,\n467\t            consecutive_race_lost: 0,\n468\t            beads_processed,\n469\t            shutdown,\n470\t            last_error: None,\n471\t            boot_time: None,\n472\t            built_prompt: None,\n473\t            current_strand: None,\n474\t            exec_output: None,\n475\t            last_effort: None,\n476\t            pre_dispatch_head: None,\n477\t            current_workspace: default_workspace,\n478\t            booting_emitted,\n479\t            last_waterfall_restarts: 0,\n480\t            last_restart_triggers: Vec::new(),\n481\t            last_strand_evaluations: Vec::new(),\n482\t            handling_state_entered_at: None,\n483\t            watchdog_triggered: watchdog_triggered.clone(),\n484\t            watchdog_handle: None,\n485\t            bead_lifecycle_span: None,\n486\t            last_outcome: None,\n487\t        }\n488\t    }\n489\t\n490\t    /// Start the watchdog thread that monitors HANDLING state duration.\n491\t    ///\n492\t    /// The watchdog runs in a separate thread (not part of the Tokio runtime)\n493\t    /// and can detect when the worker is stuck in HANDLING state even if\n494\t    /// the Tokio runtime becomes wedged. If HANDLING state exceeds the\n495\t    /// timeout, the watchdog sets the `watchdog_triggered` flag, which\n496\t    /// the main worker loop checks to force recovery.\n497\t    fn start_watchdog_thread(&mut self) {\n498\t        let watchdog_triggered = self.watchdog_triggered.clone();\n499\t        let handling_state_entered_at_ptr =\n500\t            &self.handling_state_entered_at as *const Option<Instant> as usize;\n501\t\n502\t        let handle = std::thread::spawn(move || {\n503\t            loop {\n504\t                std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_secs(5));\n505\t                if watchdog_triggered.load(Ordering::Relaxed) {\n506\t                    // Watchdog has already triggered, exit the thread.\n507\t                    break;\n508\t                }\n509\t                // Check if we've been in HANDLING state for too long.\n510\t                // We read the timestamp from the Worker struct via the pointer.\n511\t                // SAFETY: The Worker struct outlives the watchdog thread because\n512\t                // the thread is joined when the Worker is dropped.\n513\t                let entered_at = unsafe {\n514\t                    let ptr = handling_state_entered_at_ptr as *const Option<Instant>;\n515\t                    (*ptr).as_ref().copied()\n516\t                };\n517\t\n518\t                if let Some(entry_time) = entered_at {\n519\t                    let elapsed = entry_time.elapsed().as_secs();\n520\t                    if elapsed >= HANDLING_WATCHDOG_TIMEOUT_SECS {\n521\t                        tracing::error!(\n522\t                            elapsed_secs = elapsed,\n523\t                            \"HANDLING state watchdog triggered - forcing recovery\"\n524\t                        );\n525\t                        watchdog_triggered.store(true, Ordering::Release);\n526\t                        break;\n527\t                    }\n528\t                }\n529\t            }\n530\t        });\n531\t\n532\t        self.watchdog_handle = Some(handle);\n533\t    }\n534\t\n535\t    /// Run the worker loop until exhausted, stopped, or errored.\n536\t    ///\n537\t    /// The main loop is a match on `self.state`. Every state has a handler\n538\t    /// that performs its actions and sets `self.state` to the next state.\n539\t    ///\n540\t    /// Guarantees that the telemetry BufWriter is flushed before returning,\n541\t    /// even when the inner state machine exits early via `?`.\n542\t    pub async fn run(&mut self) -> Result<WorkerState> {\n543\t        // Start the telemetry writer now that we are inside the tokio runtime.\n544\t        self.telemetry.start();\n545\t\n546\t        // IMMEDIATE boot event — must be the first thing emitted after telemetry starts.\n547\t        // This ensures we get a trace even if subsequent init steps block indefinitely.\n548\t        // Skip if already emitted externally (e.g., from CLI layer for early boot diagnostics).\n549\t        if !self.booting_emitted {\n550\t            self.telemetry.emit(EventKind::WorkerBooting {\n551\t                worker_name: self.worker_name.clone(),\n552\t                version: env!(\"CARGO_PKG_VERSION\").to_string(),\n553\t            })?;\n554\t            // Force-flush to disk before boot() — if init blocks, we still have a trace.\n555\t            self.telemetry\n556\t                .force_flush_async(std::time::Duration::from_secs(5))\n557\t                .await?;\n558\t        }\n559\t\n560\t        let result = self.run_inner().await;\n561\t\n562\t        // Safety-net flush: shutdown() is idempotent. Normal terminal paths\n563\t        // (stop, handle_exhausted, Errored) already call it; this catches\n564\t        // any early-exit via `?` (boot failure, state handler panic, etc.)\n565\t        // so the BufWriter is always flushed before the tokio Runtime drops.\n566\t        self.telemetry.shutdown().await;\n567\t\n568\t        result\n569\t    }\n570\t\n571\t    /// Inner state machine — called only from [`run()`](Self::run).\n572\t    ///\n573\t    /// May return early via `?` without calling `telemetry.shutdown()`;\n574\t    /// `run()` handles the safety-net flush.\n575\t    async fn run_inner(&mut self) -> Result<WorkerState> {\n576\t        // Boot: validate config and initialize.\n577\t        self.boot()?;\n578\t\n579\t        // Start the watchdog thread that monitors HANDLING state duration.\n580\t        // This must be started after boot() so the Worker struct is fully initialized.\n581\t        self.start_watchdog_thread();\n582\t\n583\t        // Install signal handlers.\n584\t        self.install_signal_handlers();\n585\t\n586\t        // Create the worker.session root span that encompasses the entire worker lifecycle.\n587\t        let worker_id = self.qualified_id();\n588\t        let session_span = tracing::info_span!(\n589\t            \"worker.session\",\n590\t            needle.worker_id = %worker_id,\n591\t            needle.session_id = %self.telemetry.session_id(),\n592\t            needle.agent = %self.config.agent.default,\n593\t            needle.model = %self.config.agent.default, // Will be updated when adapter is resolved\n594\t            needle.workspace = %self.config.workspace.default.display(),\n595\t        );\n596\t        let _enter = session_span.enter();\n597\t\n598\t        loop {\n599\t            // Check for shutdown signal between states.\n600\t            if self.shutdown.load(Ordering::SeqCst) {\n601\t                // Retrieve and clear the last received signal for logging.\n602\t                #[cfg(unix)]\n603\t                let signal_name = {\n604\t                    let sig = LAST_SIGNAL.swap(0, AtomicOrdering::SeqCst);\n605\t                    if sig == 0 {\n606\t                        None\n607\t                    } else {\n608\t                        Some(match sig {\n609\t                            1 => \"SIGHUP\",\n610\t                            2 => \"SIGINT\",\n611\t                            15 => \"SIGTERM\",\n612\t                            _ => \"unknown signal\",\n613\t                        })\n614\t                    }\n615\t                };\n616\t                #[cfg(not(unix))]\n617\t                let signal_name = None;\n618\t\n619\t                let reason = if let Some(name) = signal_name {\n620\t                    format!(\"signal received ({name})\")\n621\t                } else {\n622\t                    \"signal received\".to_string()\n623\t                };\n624\t\n625\t                match self.state {\n626\t                    // If we're in the middle of processing a bead, handle it\n627\t                    // as interrupted so the bead gets released.\n628\t                    WorkerState::Building\n629\t                    | WorkerState::Dispatching\n630\t                    | WorkerState::Executing\n631\t                    | WorkerState::Handling => {\n632\t                        // Let the current state handler finish, but mark\n633\t                        // that we should stop after handling.\n634\t                    }\n635\t                    // For states where we don't hold a bead, stop immediately.\n636\t                    WorkerState::Selecting\n637\t                    | WorkerState::Claiming\n638\t                    | WorkerState::Retrying\n639\t                    | WorkerState::Logging => {\n640\t                        // Release any claimed bead before stopping.\n641\t                        if let Some(ref bead) = self.current_bead {\n642\t                            let bead_id = bead.id.clone();\n643\t                            tracing::info!(bead_id = %bead_id, \"releasing bead on shutdown\");\n644\t                            let _ = self.store.release(&bead_id).await;\n645\t                        }\n646\t                        return self.stop(&reason).await;\n647\t                    }\n648\t                    WorkerState::Stopped | WorkerState::Exhausted | WorkerState::Errored => {\n649\t                        return self.stop(&reason).await;\n650\t                    }\n651\t                    WorkerState::Booting => {\n652\t                        return self.stop(\"signal received during boot\").await;\n653\t                    }\n654\t                }\n655\t            }\n656\t\n657\t            // Check for watchdog trigger - this indicates HANDLING state is wedged.\n658\t            // The watchdog runs in a separate thread and can detect when the worker\n659\t            // is stuck even if the Tokio runtime becomes unresponsive.\n660\t            if self.watchdog_triggered.load(Ordering::Relaxed)\n661\t                && self.state == WorkerState::Handling\n662\t            {\n663\t                tracing::error!(\"watchdog detected HANDLING state hang, forcing recovery\");\n664\t                // Emit critical timeout event.\n665\t                let bead_id = self.current_bead.as_ref().map(|b| b.id.clone());\n666\t                let _ = self\n667\t                    .telemetry\n668\t                    .emit_try_lock(EventKind::WorkerHandlingTimeout {\n669\t                        bead_id: bead_id.clone().unwrap_or_else(|| BeadId::from(\"unknown\")),\n670\t                        outcome: \"unknown\".to_string(),\n671\t                        operation: \"watchdog\".to_string(),\n672\t                        error: format!(\n673\t                            \"HANDLING state exceeded {}s timeout\",\n674\t                            HANDLING_WATCHDOG_TIMEOUT_SECS\n675\t                        ),\n676\t                    });\n677\t                // Attempt best-effort release if we have a bead.\n678\t                if let Some(ref bead) = self.current_bead {\n679\t                    let bead_id = bead.id.clone();\n680\t                    tracing::warn!(bead_id = %bead_id, \"best-effort bead release due to watchdog timeout\");\n681\t                    let _ =\n682\t                        tokio::time::timeout(Duration::from_secs(30), self.store.release(&bead_id))\n683\t                            .await;\n684\t                }\n685\t                // Clear the watchdog trigger and force transition to LOGGING.\n686\t                self.watchdog_triggered.store(false, Ordering::Release);\n687\t                self.handling_state_entered_at = None;\n688\t                // Force transition to LOGGING to recover.\n689\t                self.set_state(WorkerState::Logging)?;\n690\t            }\n691\t\n692\t            match self.state {\n693\t                WorkerState::Selecting => self.do_select().await?,\n694\t                WorkerState::Claiming => self.do_claim().await?,\n695\t                WorkerState::Retrying => self.do_retry().await?,\n696\t                WorkerState::Building => self.do_build().await?,\n697\t                WorkerState::Dispatching => self.do_dispatch().await?,\n698\t                WorkerState::Executing => self.do_execute().await?,\n699\t                WorkerState::Handling => self.do_handle().await?,\n700\t                WorkerState::Logging => self.do_log()?,\n701\t                WorkerState::Exhausted => {\n702\t                    let next = self.handle_exhausted().await?;\n703\t                    match next {\n704\t                        WorkerState::Selecting => continue,\n705\t                        terminal => return Ok(terminal),\n706\t                    }\n707\t                }\n708\t                WorkerState::Stopped => {\n709\t                    return self.stop(\"normal shutdown\").await;\n710\t                }\n711\t                WorkerState::Errored => {\n712\t                    let err = self\n713\t                        .last_error\n714\t                        .take()\n715\t                        .unwrap_or_else(|| anyhow::anyhow!(\"unknown error\"));\n716\t                    let msg = format!(\"{err}\");\n717\t                    self.telemetry.emit(EventKind::WorkerErrored {\n718\t                        error_type: \"worker_scoped\".to_string(),\n719\t                        error_message: msg.clone(),\n720\t                        beads_processed: self.beads_processed,\n721\t                    })?;\n722\t\n723\t                    // Emit WorkerStopped before exiting so telemetry shows a clean shutdown.\n724\t                    // This ensures operators can distinguish \"exited with error\" from\n725\t                    // \"killed by external agent\" (e.g., SIGKILL, OOM).\n726\t                    let uptime = self.boot_time.map(|t| t.elapsed().as_secs()).unwrap_or(0);\n727\t                    let _ = self.telemetry.emit(EventKind::WorkerStopped {\n728\t                        reason: format!(\"error: {msg}\"),\n729\t                        beads_processed: self.beads_processed,\n730\t                        uptime_secs: uptime,\n731\t                    });\n732\t\n733\t                    // Best-effort stop heartbeat on error.\n734\t                    self.health.stop();\n735\t                    // Best-effort deregister on error.\n736\t                    if let Err(e) = self.registry.deregister(&self.qualified_id()) {\n737\t                        tracing::warn!(error = %e, \"failed to deregister from worker registry on error\");\n738\t                    }\n739\t                    self.telemetry.shutdown().await;\n740\t                    return Err(err);\n741\t                }\n742\t                WorkerState::Booting => {\n743\t                    bail!(\"boot() should have transitioned past Booting state\");\n744\t                }\n745\t            }\n746\t        }\n747\t    }\n748\t\n749\t    // ── Boot ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n750\t\n751\t    /// Validate configuration and initialize the worker.\n752\t    ///\n753\t    /// Each step is instrumented with `init.step.started`/`init.step.completed`\n754\t    /// events so that hangs are visible in the telemetry log. Boot duration is\n755\t    /// capped at 60 seconds — if exceeded, the worker self-aborts with a\n756\t    /// `worker.boot.timeout` event and exits with a non-zero code.\n757\t    fn boot(&mut self) -> Result<()> {\n758\t        self.boot_time = Some(Instant::now());\n759\t        const BOOT_TIMEOUT_SECS: u64 = 60;\n760\t\n761\t        // Step: Config validation\n762\t        self.telemetry.emit(EventKind::InitStepStarted {\n763\t            step: \"config_validation\".to_string(),\n764\t        })?;\n765\t        let step_start = Instant::now();\n766\t        let errors = ConfigLoader::validate(&self.config);\n767\t        if !errors.is_empty() {\n768\t            let msg = errors\n769\t                .iter()\n770\t                .map(|e| e.to_string())\n771\t                .collect::<Vec<_>>()\n772\t                .join(\"; \");\n773\t            bail!(\"config validation failed: {msg}\");\n774\t        }\n775\t        self.telemetry.emit(EventKind::InitStepCompleted {\n776\t            step: \"config_validation\".to_string(),\n777\t            duration_ms: step_start.elapsed().as_millis() as u64,\n778\t        })?;\n779\t\n780\t        // Check boot timeout before each step\n781\t        self.check_boot_timeout(BOOT_TIMEOUT_SECS)?;\n782\t\n783\t        // Step: Registry registration\n784\t        self.telemetry.emit(EventKind::InitStepStarted {\n785\t            step: \"registry_registration\".to_string(),\n786\t        })?;\n787\t        let step_start = Instant::now();\n788\t        let `worker.boot.timeout` and exits the process with a non-zero\n843\t    /// code. This is a last-resort measure when an init step hangs.\n844\t    fn check_boot_timeout(&self, timeout_secs: u64) -> Result<()> {\n845\t        if let Some(boot_start) = self.boot_time {\n846\t            let elapsed = boot_start.elapsed();\n847\t            if elapsed.as_secs() > timeout_secs {\n848\t                let elapsed_ms = elapsed.as_millis() as u64;\n849\t                // Emit the timeout event before aborting\n850\t                let _ = self\n851\t                    .telemetry\n852\t                    .emit(EventKind::WorkerBootTimeout { elapsed_ms });\n853\t                tracing::error!(\n854\t                    elapsed_ms,\n855\t                    \"boot timeout exceeded {}s — aborting\",\n856\t                    timeout_secs\n857\t                );\n858\t                // Flush telemetry before exit\n859\t                std::mem::forget(self.telemetry.clone());\n860\t                // Exit with a distinct code to indicate boot timeout\n861\t                std::process::exit(71); // EX_OSERR + custom offset\n862\t            }\n863\t        }\n864\t        Ok(())\n865\t    }\n866\t\n867\t    // ── Signal handling ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n868\t\n869\t    /// Install SIGINT, SIGTERM, and SIGHUP handlers that set the shutdown flag.\n870\t    ///\n871\t    /// SIGHUP is handled because when the parent bash dies (e.g., tmux session\n872\t    /// killed, external reaper), the child process receives SIGHUP by default.\n873\t    /// Without a handler, the process terminates immediately without emitting\n874\t    /// worker.stopped telemetry or flushing the telemetry buffer.\n875\t    ///\n876\t    /// Uses synchronous signal handlers via libc/signal-hook to ensure signals\n877\t    /// are caught immediately, even if the tokio runtime hasn't polled async\n878\t    /// signal tasks yet. This prevents silent process termination when signals\n879\t    /// arrive early (e.g., SIGHUP from parent bash death during worker startup).\n880\t    fn install_signal_handlers(&self) {\n881\t        // Store a global reference to the shutdown flag for signal handlers.\n882\t        // We use a leak to ensure the reference lives for the entire program duration.\n883\t        let shutdown_ptr = Arc::into_raw(self.shutdown.clone()) as usize;\n884\t        set_global_shutdown_flag(shutdown_ptr);\n885\t\n886\t        // Register atexit handler to emit worker.stopped telemetry on unexpected termination.\n887\t        // This provides diagnostic information when the worker is killed by an external\n888\t        // process (e.g., capacity governor, OOM killer, SIGKILL).\n889\t        let start_time = self.boot_time.unwrap_or_else(Instant::now);\n890\t        register_atexit_handler(\n891\t            self.worker_name.clone(),\n892\t            self.beads_processed,\n893\t            start_time,\n894\t            format!(\"{:?}\", self.state),\n895\t            None, // log_file_path - RETRYING: decide whether to retry claiming or move on.\n1222\t    ///\n1223\t    /// Tracks consecutive race_lost across retry cycles. When the count\n1224\t    /// exceeds `claim_race_lost_skip`, the worker treats the ready queue\n1225\t    /// as effectively empty and transitions to Exhausted instead of\n1226\t    /// spinning indefinitely on the same bead.\n1227\t    async fn do_retry(&mut self) -> Result<()> {\n1228\t        let skip_threshold = self.config.worker.claim_race_lost_skip;\n1229\t\n1230\t        if self.consecutive_race_lost >= skip_threshold {\n1231\t            tracing::warn!(\n1232\t                consecutive_race_lost = self.consecutive_race_lost,\n1233\t                threshold = skip_threshold,\n1234\t                \"consecutive race_lost exceeded skip threshold, treating queue as exhausted\"\n1235\t            );\n1236\t            self.telemetry.emit(EventKind::ClaimRaceLostSkipped {\n1237\t                consecutive_losses: self.consecutive_race_lost,\n1238\t                threshold: skip_threshold,\n1239\t            })?;\n1240\t            self.consecutive_race_lost = 0;\n1241\t            self.retry_count = 0;\n1242\t            self.exclusion_set.clear();\n1243\t            self.race_lost_this_cycle.clear();\n1244\t            self.current_bead = None;\n1245\t            self.set_state(WorkerState::Exhausted)?;\n1246\t            return Ok(());\n1247\t        }\n1248\t\n1249\t        // Exponential backoff: start at 100ms, doubling each time, capped at 5s.\n1250\t        // This ensures even the first retry has a small delay to prevent tight loops.\n1251\t        let backoff_ms = if self.consecutive_race_lost > 0 {\n1252\t            // For race-lost retries: 100ms, 200ms, 400ms, 800ms, 1600ms, 3200ms, 5000ms (capped)\n1253\t            std::cmp::min(\n1254\t                100 * (1u64 << (self.consecutive_race_lost - 1).min(5)),\n1255\t                5000,\n1256\t            )\n1257\t        } else {\n1258\t            // For other retries (e.g., max_claim_retries): 100ms minimum\n1259\t            100\n1260\t        };\n1261\t        tracing::debug!(\n1262\t            consecutive_race_lost = self.consecutive_race_lost,\n1263\t            backoff_ms,\n1264\t            \"backing off before retry\"\n1265\t        );\n1266\t        tokio::time::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_millis(backoff_ms)).await;\n1267\t\n1268\t        if self.retry_count < self.config.worker.max_claim_retries {\n1269\t            self.set_state(WorkerState::Selecting)?;\n1270\t        } else {\n1271\t            tracing::debug!(\n1272\t                retry_count = self.retry_count,\n1273\t                \"max claim retries exceeded, clearing retry state for next cycle\"\n1274\t            );\n1275\t            self.retry_count = 0;\n1276\t            self.consecutive_race_lost = 0;\n1277\t            self.exclusion_set.clear();\n1278\t            self.race_lost_this_cycle.clear();\n1279\t            // NOTE: Do NOT clear race_lost_exclusions here. Those have TTL-based\n1280\t            // expiration and must persist to prevent re-selecting the same bead\n1281\t            // that just lost a claim race. self.set_state(WorkerState::Dispatching)?;\n1425\t        Ok(())\n1426\t    }\n1427\t\n1428\t    /// DISPATCHING: check rate limits, resolve adapter, and prepare for execution.\n1429\t    async fn    }\n1646\t        };\n1647\t\n1648\t        // Emit an initial heartbeat event to signal we've entered HANDLING state.\n1649\t        // This provides immediate visibility in the JSONL log when self.last_outcome = Some(result.outcome.as_str().to_string());\n1740\t\n1741\t                    // Set span status: Ok for Success, Error for all other outcomes.\n1742\t                    match result.outcome {\n1743\t                        crate::types::Outcome::Success => {\n1744\t                            // Span status is Ok by default\n1745\t                        }\n1746\t retention_days,\n144\t            traces_dir,\n145\t            trace_retention_failed_days,\n146\t            trace_retention_success_days,\n147\t            config.workspace.default.clone(),\n148\t            config.strands.learning.max_learnings,\n149\t            config.workspace.home.join(\"state\"),\n150\t            config.limits.clone(),\n151\t        );\n152\t\n153\t        let state_base KnotStrand::new(config.strands.knot.clone());\n218\t        StrandRunner {\n219\t            strands: vec![\n220\t                Box::new(pluck),\n221\t                Box::new(mend),\n222\t                Box::new(explore),\n223\t                Box::new(weave),\n224\t                Box::new(unravel),\n225\t                Box::new(pulse),\n226\t                Box::new(reflect),\n227\t                Box::new(splice),\n228\t                Box::new(knot),\n229\t            ],\n230\t            telemetry: runner_telemetry,\n231\t        }\n232\t    }\n233\t\n234\t    /// Run the waterfall, returning a `SelectOutcome` that carries the winning\n235\t    /// candidate (if any) plus restart diagnostics.\n236\t    ///\n237\t    /// Returns the full `Bead` (including its workspace path) so the caller\n238\t    /// can create the correct bead store for remote beads found by Explore.\n239\t    /// The accompanying `String` is the name of the strand that produced the\n240\t    /// candidate.\n241\t    ///\n242\t    /// When a strand returns `WorkCreated`, the waterfall restarts from Pluck.\n243\t    /// A restart cap prevents infinite loops (e.g. a strand that always creates\n244\t    /// work without producing a claimable bead).\n245\t    pub async fn select(\n246\t        &self,\n247\t        store: &dyn BeadStore,\n248\t        exclusions: &HashSet<BeadId>,\n249\t    ) -> Result<SelectOutcome> {\n250\t        const MAX_RESTARTS: u32 = 3;\n251\t        let mut restarts = 0u32;\n252\t        let mut restart_triggers: Vec<String> = Vec::new();\n253\t        let mut strand_evaluations: Vec<StrandEvaluation> = Vec::new();\n254\t\n255\t        'waterfall: loop {\n256\t            for strand in &self.strands {\n257\t                let strand_name = strand.name().to_string();\n258\t                let strand_span = tracing::info_span!(\n259\t                    \"strand.{}\",\n260\t                    strand_name,\n261\t                    needle.strand.name = %strand_name,\n262\t                );\n263\t                let strand_enter = strand_span.entered();\n264\t\n265\t                let start = Instant::now();\n266\t                let result = strand.evaluate(store).await;\n267\t                let elapsed_ms = start.elapsed().as_millis() as u64;\n268\t\n269\t                // Record strand evaluation result as span attribute.\n270\t                let (result_str, should_record) = match &result {\n271\t                    StrandResult::BeadFound(beads) => {\n272\t                        let count = beads.len();\n273\t                        (\n274\t                            format!(\"{}({})\", strand_results::BEAD_FOUND, count),\n275\t                            count > 0,\n276\t                        )\n277\t                    }\n278\t                    StrandResult::WorkCreated => (strand_results::WORK_CREATED.to_string(), true),\n279\t                    StrandResult::NoWork => (strand_results::NO_WORK.to_string(), true),\n280\t                    StrandResult::Error(_) => (strand_results::ERROR.to_string(), true),\n281\t                    StrandResult::Split(_, _failure_count) => {\n282\t                        (format!(\"{}({})\", strand_results::BEAD_FOUND, 1), true)\n283\t                    }\n284\t                };\n285\t                tracing::Span::current().record(attrs::NEEDLE_STRAND_RESULT, &result_str);\n286\t                tracing::Span::current().record(attrs::NEEDLE_STRAND_DURATION_MS, elapsed_ms);\n287\t\n288\t                // Set strand span status: Error for strand errors, Ok for all other results\n289\t                if matches!(result, StrandResult::Error(_)) {\n290\t                    tracing::Span::current().record(\"otel.status_code\", 2u64);\n291\t                    tracing::Span::current().record(\"otel.status_description\", &result_str);\n292\t                }\n293\t\n294\t                // Only record evaluations that produced meaningful results.\n295\t                // Skip recording empty BeadFound results since they don't\n296\t                // represent actual strand activity.\n297\t                if should_record {\n298\t                    strand_evaluations.push(StrandEvaluation {\n299\t                        strand_name: strand_name.clone(),\n300\t                        result: result_str.clone(),\n301\t                        duration_ms: elapsed_ms,\n302\t                    });\n303\t                }\n304\t\n305\t                match result {\n306\t                    StrandResult::BeadFound(beads) => {\n307\t                        // Filter out beads that are in the exclusion set (e.g.\n308\t                        // recently race-lost).  This prevents the waterfall from\n309\t                        // immediately re-selecting a bead that just lost a claim\n310\t                        // race to another worker.\n311\t                        let original_count = beads.len();\n312\t                        let filtered: Vec<Bead> = beads\n313\t                            .into_iter()\n314\t                            .filter(|b| !exclusions.contains(&b.id))\n315\t                            .collect();\n316\t                        let excluded_count = original_count.saturating_sub(filtered.len());\n317\t\n318\t                        // Record queue depth for the Pluck strand (after filtering).\n319\t                        // This samples the current queue depth for the needle.queue.depth\n320\t                        // observable gauge, which is measured at strand evaluation.\n321\t                        // We report depth per priority level to enable filtered views.\n322\t                        if strand_name == \"pluck\" {\n323\t                            use std::collections::HashMap;\n324\t                            let mut depths: HashMap<u8, u64> = HashMap::new();\n325\t                            for bead in &filtered {\n326\t                                *depths.entry(bead.priority).or_insert(0) += 1;\n327\t                            }\n328\t                            self.telemetry.record_queue_depth(depths);\n329\t                        }\n330\t\n331\t                        if let Err(e) =\n332\t                            self.telemetry\n333\t                                .emit(crate::telemetry::EventKind::StrandEvaluated {\n334\t                                    strand_name: strand_name.clone(),\n335\t                                    result: \"bead_found\".to_string(),\n336\t                                    duration_ms: elapsed_ms,\n337\t                                })\n338\t                        {\n339\t                            tracing::warn!(\n340\t                                strand = %strand_name,\n341\t                                error = %e,\n342\t                                \"failed to emit strand evaluated telemetry\"\n343\t                            );\n344\t                        }\n345\t                        tracing::info!(\n346\t                            strand = %strand_name,\n347\t                            candidates = filtered.len(),\n348\t                            excluded = excluded_count,\n349\t                            elapsed_ms,\n350\t                            \"strand found candidates\"\n351\t                        );\n352\t                        if let Some(bead) = filtered.into_iter().next() {\n353\t                            // Keep the strand span active through the bead lifecycle.\n354\t                            // The span guard will be dropped when the bead lifecycle ends.\n355\t                            return Ok(SelectOutcome {\n356\t                                bead: Some((bead, strand_name.clone())),\n357\t                                waterfall_restarts: restarts,\n358\t                                restart_triggers,\n359\t                                strand_evaluations,\n360\t                                strand_span_guard: Some(strand_enter),\n361\t                                split_failure_count: None,\n362\t                            });\n363\t                        }\n364\t                        continue;\n365\t                    }\n366\t                    StrandResult::Split(bead, failure_count) => {\n367\t                        let bead = *bead;\n368\t                        if let Err(e) =\n369\t                            self.telemetry\n370\t                                .emit(crate::telemetry::EventKind::StrandEvaluated {\n371\t                                    strand_name: strand_name.clone(),\n372\t                                    result: \"split\".to_string(),\n373\t                                    duration_ms: elapsed_ms,\n374\t                                })\n375\t                        {\n376\t                            tracing::warn!(\n377\t                                strand = %strand_name,\n378\t                                error = %e,\n379\t                                \"failed to emit strand evaluated telemetry\"\n380\t                            );\n381\t                        }\n382\t                        tracing::info!(\n383\t                            strand = %strand_name,\n384\t                            bead_id = %bead.id,\n385\t                            failure_count,\n386\t                            \"strand triggered split for bead with excessive failures\"\n387\t                        );\n388\t                        return Ok(SelectOutcome {\n389\t                            bead: Some((bead, strand_name.clone())),\n390\t                            waterfall_restarts: restarts,\n391\t                            restart_triggers,\n392\t                            strand_evaluations,\n393\t                            strand_span_guard: Some(strand_enter),\n394\t                            split_failure_count: Some(failure_count),\n395\t                        });\n396\t                    }\n397\t                    StrandResult::WorkCreated => {\n398\t                        if let Err(e) =\n399\t                            self.telemetry\n400\t                                .emit(crate::telemetry::EventKind::StrandEvaluated {\n401\t                                    strand_name: strand_name.clone(),\n402\t                                    result: \"work_created\".to_string(),\n403\t                                    duration_ms: elapsed_ms,\n404\t                                })\n405\t                        {\n406\t                            tracing::warn!(\n407\t                                strand = %strand_name,\n408\t                                error = %e,\n409\t                                \"failed to emit strand evaluated telemetry\"\n410\t                            );\n411\t                        }\n412\t                        restarts += 1;\n413\t                        restart_triggers.push(strand_name.clone());\n414\t                        if restarts > MAX_RESTARTS {\n415\t                            tracing::warn!(\n416\t                                strand = %strand_name,\n417\t                                max_restarts = MAX_RESTARTS,\n418\t                                \"waterfall restart cap reached, continuing to evaluate remaining strands\"\n419\t                            );\n420\t                            // Do NOT return None — continue evaluating remaining strands\n421\t                            // so every strand emits telemetry and the operator can see\n422\t                            // why the worker is idle.\n423\t                            continue;\n424\t                        }\n425\t                        tracing::info!(\n426\t                            strand = %strand_name,\n427\t                            elapsed_ms,\n428\t                            restart = restarts,\n429\t                            \"strand created new work, restarting waterfall\"\n430\t                        );\n431\t                        continue 'waterfall;\n432\t                    }\n433\t                    StrandResult::NoWork => {\n434\t                        if let Err(e) =\n435\t                            self.telemetry\n436\t                                .emit(crate::telemetry::EventKind::StrandEvaluated {\n437\t                                    strand_name: strand_name.clone(),\n438\t                                    result: \"no_work\".to_string(),\n439\t                                    duration_ms: elapsed_ms,\n440\t                                })\n441\t                        {\n442\t                            tracing::warn!(\n443\t                                strand = %strand_name,\n444\t                                error = %e,\n445\t                                \"failed to emit strand evaluated telemetry\"\n446\t                            );\n447\t                        }\n448\t                        tracing::info!(\n449\t                            strand = %strand_name,\n450\t                            elapsed_ms,\n451\t                            \"strand returned no work\"\n452\t                        );\n453\t                        continue;\n454\t                    }\n455\t                    StrandResult::Error(e) => {\n456\t                        if let Err(te) =\n457\t                            self.telemetry\n458\t                                .emit(crate::telemetry::EventKind::StrandEvaluated {\n459\t                                    strand_name: strand_name.clone(),\n460\t                                    result: \"error\".to_string(),\n461\t                                    duration_ms: elapsed_ms,\n462\t                                })\n463\t                        {\n464\t                            tracing::warn!(\n465\t                                strand = %strand_name,\n466\t                                error = %te,\n467\t                                \"failed to emit strand evaluated telemetry\"\n468\t                            );\n469\t                        }\n470\t                        tracing::warn!(\n471\t                            strand = %strand_name,\n472\t                            error = %e,\n473\t                            elapsed_ms,\n474\t                            \"strand error, continuing to next strand\"\n475\t                        );\n476\t                        continue;\n477\t                    }\n478\t                }\n479\t            }\n480\t            // All strands evaluated without finding work or triggering a restart.\n481\t            return Ok(SelectOutcome {\n482\t                bead: None,\n483\t                waterfall_restarts: restarts,\n484\t                restart_triggers,\n485\t                strand_evaluations,\n486\t                strand_span_guard: None,\n487\t                split_failure_count: None,\n488\t            });\n489\t        }\n490\t    }\n491\t\n492\t    /// Return the names of all configured strands (for telemetry/debugging).\n493\t    pub fn strand_names(&self) -> Vec<&str> {\n494\t        self.strands.iter().map(|s| s.name()).collect()\n495\t    }\n496\t}\n497\t\n498\t// ─── Unit tests ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n499\t\n500\t#[cfg(test)]\n501\tmod tests {\n502\t    use super::*;\n503\t    use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};\n18\t\n19\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n20\t// PID liveness checking (platform-specific)\n21\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n22\t\n23\t/// Check if a process with the given PID is currently running.\n24\t///\n25\t/// Returns `false` if the PID does not exist or if we lack permission to signal it.\n26\t/// This is best-effort: if we can't determine liveness, we assume the process is dead\n27\t/// to avoid counting stale entries toward concurrency limits.\n28\tpub fn is_pid_alive(pid: u32) -> bool {\n29\t    #[cfg(unix)]\n30\t    {\n31\t        // Use libc::kill with signal 0 to check if process exists.\n32\t        // SAFETY: kill(pid, 0) only checks existence, doesn't send a signal.\n33\t        unsafe {\n34\t            let ret = libc::kill(pid as i32, 0);\n35\t            if ret == 0 {\n36\t                // kill succeeded: process exists and we have permission to signal it.\n37\t                return true;\n38\t            }\n39\t            // kill failed: check errno to distinguish EPERM from ESRCH.\n40\t            // We must read errno immediately after kill, before any other syscalls.\n41\t            let errno = *libc::__errno_location();\n42\t            match errno {\n43\t                libc::ESRCH => {\n44\t                    // No such process: PID is dead.\n45\t                    false\n46\t                }\n47\t                libc::EPERM => {\n48\t                    // Process exists but we don't have permission to signal it.\n49\t                    // We treat this as \"alive\" since the process actually exists.\n50\t                    true\n51\t                }\n52\t                _ => {\n53\t                    // Other errors (e.g., EINVAL for invalid signal).\n54\t                    // Conservatively treat as dead to avoid false positives.\n55\t                    false\n56\t                }\n57\t            }\n58\t        }\n59\t    }\n60\t\n61\t    #[cfg(not(unix))]\n62\t    {\n63\t        // On non-Unix platforms, conservatively return false (assume dead).\n64\t        // This prevents false positives where dead PIDs are counted as live.\n65\t        // TODO: Implement Windows liveness check via OpenProcess if needed.\n66\t        false\n67\t    }\n68\t}\n69\t\n70\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n71\t// WorkerEntry\n72\t// {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"call_19c949197ee9406cb21a073c","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t# Operational Fleet Lessons\n2\t\n3\tExtracted from memory files, bead history, git log patterns, and the\n4\tbead-splitting and gap-analysis docs in NEEDLE-deprecated.\n5\t\n6\t## Fleet Sizing and Capacity\n7\t\n8\t### Worker Limits\n9\t\n10\tThe server (Hetzner EX44, 20 cores) could not sustain more than ~20\n11\tconcurrent NEEDLE workers. At 40+ workers, the explore strand's filesystem\n12\tscans drove CPU load to 35+, degrading all workers.\n13\t\n14\t**Lesson:** NEEDLE's worker count must be bounded by the server's capacity\n15\tfor overhead operations (filesystem scans, database queries, heartbeats),\n16\tnot just the number of available beads.\n17\t\n18\t### Staggered Launches\n19\t\n20\tLaunching all workers simultaneously caused a thundering herd effect:\n21\t- All workers hit explore at the same time\n22\t- All workers tried to claim the same top-priority bead\n23\t- Database lock contention spiked\n24\t\n25\t**Mitigation:** Stagger launches with `sleep 1-2` between each worker.\n26\tThe rewrite should have built-in stagger logic.\n27\t\n28\t### Workers Exit When Done\n29\t\n30\tWorkers complete their workspace's beads and then exit via `idle_timeout`.\n31\tThis is normal behavior, not a crash. Operators must periodically relaunch\n32\tworkers into workspaces with remaining work.\n33\t\n34\t**Lesson:** The system needs either: (a) automatic worker redistribution\n35\tto busy workspaces, or (b) a supervisor that relaunches workers when work\n36\tappears.\n37\t\n38\t## Bead Granularity\n39\t\n40\t### The Splitting Report Findings\n41\t\n42\tThe bead-splitting-report.md found that 4 oversized beads (>4,000 characters,\n43\t10+ acceptance criteria) had higher failure rates. After splitting into 13\n44\tfocused beads, timeout risk decreased and parallel work increased.\n45\t\n46\t**Guidelines established:**\n47\t- Split if >4,000 characters or >10 acceptance criteria\n48\t- Split if bead mixes concerns (CLI + execution + recovery)\n49\t- Split if sequential phases (\"first X, then Y, then Z\")\n50\t- Keep together if single file/module, <3,000 characters, <8 criteria\n51\t\n52\t**Common pattern: three-part split:**\n53\t1. Setup/infrastructure/framework (P0)\n54\t2. Core implementation/execution (P0)\n55\t3. Advanced features/cleanup/recovery (P1/P2)\n56\t\n57\t### Timeout Escalations\n58\t\n59\t~20% of closed beads had timeout escalations. Oversized beads were the\n60\tprimary cause -- the LLM agent could not complete all acceptance criteria\n61\twithin the timeout.\n62\t\n63\t## The Gap Analysis Cycle\n64\t\n65\t### First Pass (2026-03-07)\n66\t\n67\tThe gap analysis (nd-12nu) compared implementation against `docs/plan.md`\n68\tand found 14 gaps across CLI commands, strands, hooks, watchdog, telemetry,\n69\tself-update, billing, and infrastructure. 17 new beads were created.\n70\t\n71\t### Second Pass (2026-03-08)\n72\t\n73\tMany first-pass beads were completed. A second pass found 5 new gaps\n74\tincluding auto-init detection, workspace-level config overrides, and file\n75\tcollision reconciliation.\n76\t\n77\t**Lesson:** Gap analysis is valuable but must be repeated. The first pass\n78\tcatches obvious gaps; subsequent passes catch gaps revealed by implementing\n79\tthe first batch. However, this creates a feedback loop when running on the\n80\tNEEDLE workspace itself (see self-modification-risks.md).\n81\t\n82\t## Version Management\n83\t\n84\t### The Version Bump Problem\n85\t\n86\tThe git log shows 30+ `chore: bump version` commits, each paired with a\n87\tfeature or fix commit. Version management was manual and error-prone:\n88\t- `constants.sh` version could drift from the release tag\n89\t- The build script had to sync the version from constants.sh\n90\t- CI auto-bump was added (commit 82ade80) but late in the project\n91\t\n92\t**Lesson:** Version management should be automated from day one with a\n93\tsingle source of truth.\n94\t\n95\t### Hot-Reload Deployment\n96\t\n97\tHot-reload (commits ff63d1d, 67dcbff, 2a96b8a) automatically deployed new\n98\tversions to running workers. While useful for rapid iteration, it amplified\n99\tbad changes (see self-modification-risks.md).\n100\t\n101\t## The Bead Lifecycle Observation\n102\t\n103\t### Agent-Owned Closure\n104\t\n105\tThe most important operational lesson was that bead closure should be the\n106\tagent's responsibility, not NEEDLE's post-dispatch lifecycle:\n107\t\n108\t**Problem:** NEEDLE's post-dispatch parsing (`exit_code|duration|output_file`\n109\tfrom stream-parser.sh) failed silently, orphaning beads as `in_progress`.\n110\t\n111\t**Solution:** Include instructions in the agent prompt for the LLM to:\n112\t1. Do the work\n113\t2. Commit and push\n114\t3. Validate the result against the bead spec\n115\t4. `br close <id>` if valid\n116\t5. `br update <id> --status blocked` with a comment if not\n117\t\n118\tNEEDLE's post-dispatch closure became a fallback safety net, not the primary\n119\tmechanism.\n120\t\n121\t## Provider-Specific Issues\n122\t\n123\t### ZhipuAI (GLM) Proxy Errors (nd-f0vywe)\n124\t\n125\tGLM workers through the zai-proxy encountered 422 errors from incompatible\n126\tAnthropic API fields. The proxy needed field translation for the ZhipuAI\n127\tbackend.\n128\t\n129\t### Heartbeat Format Mismatch\n130\t\n131\tGLM-4.7 workers showed as `unknown (idle)` in `needle list` due to\n132\theartbeat format differences. They were actually running. Operators had to\n133\tcheck tmux directly.\n134\t\n135\t**Lesson:** Multi-provider support requires careful abstraction of agent\n136\toutput formats, heartbeat conventions, and error handling.\n137\t\n138\t## The Plan vs. Reality\n139\t\n140\t### Feature Coverage (from plan-vs-beads-analysis.md)\n141\t\n142\tAt analysis time:\n143\t- **Phase 1 (MVP):** 60% complete, 4 critical beads open\n144\t- **Phase 2 (Full System):** 75% complete, 2 in progress\n145\t- **Phase 3 (Advanced):** 30% complete, 2 open + 5 missing\n146\t\n147\tTesting infrastructure had 0% coverage (no beads). Documentation was\n148\ttracked informally.\n149\t\n150\t### What Completed vs. What Did Not\n151\t\n152\tSuccessfully implemented: CLI commands (100% tracked), all 7 strands,\n153\tcore bead management, agent system, telemetry, configuration, process\n154\tmanagement.\n155\t\n156\tNever fully stabilized: mitosis (multiple explosion incidents), explore\n157\t(unbounded scanning), starvation detection (100% false positive rate),\n158\tbuild integrity (recurring module-missing bugs).\n159\t\n160\t**Lesson:** The features that worked well were simple, isolated modules\n161\t(CLI commands, single-strand implementations). The features that caused\n162\tproblems were cross-cutting concerns that interacted with multiple\n163\tsubsystems (mitosis touches claiming, labels, database, LLM; explore\n164\ttouches filesystem, workspace switching, engine restart; starvation\n165\ttouches all strands plus alerting).\n166\t\n167\t## Source Evidence\n168\t\n169\t- `/home/coding/NEEDLE-deprecated/docs/bead-splitting-report.md`\n170\t- `/home/coding/NEEDLE-deprecated/docs/gap-analysis-nd-12nu.md`\n171\t- `/home/coding/NEEDLE-deprecated/docs/plan-vs-beads-analysis.md`\n172\t- `/home/coding/NEEDLE-deprecated/docs/streaming.md`\n173\t- Memory: `feedback_needle_operations.md` -- fleet ops lessons\n174\t- Memory: `feedback_needle_bead_lifecycle.md` -- agent-owned closure\n175\t- Memory: `project_needle_bugs_fixed.md` -- bugs fixed 2026-03-18\n176\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":"call_9a89d6e034ef4a96b86bd0ef","session_id":"a89d22d4-b381-4e40-a7a5-355113b4a5c7","uuid":"c165fa60-087f-454c-8ade-15a3278b8f24","timestamp":"2026-06-24T12:23:00.150Z","subagent_type":"Explore","task_description":"Explore NEEDLE worker architecture"}

Worker Dispatching Architecture\n\n### Current Worker Launch Mechanism (`src/cli/mod.rs`)\n- **CLI-driven launching**: Workers are launched via `needle run` command with configurable count\n- **TMUX isolation**: Each worker runs in its own tmux session for visibility and management\n- **NATO alphabet naming**: Workers are automatically named (alpha, bravo, charlie, etc.)\n- **Staggered launches**: Workers are launched with delays to prevent thundering herd\n- **Inner invocation pattern**: Uses `NEEDLE_INNER=1` env var to detect re-entrant worker processes\n\n### Worker Lifecycle (`src/worker/mod.rs`)\nThe worker is a comprehensive state machine with explicit states:\n- **Booting**: Configuration validation and initialization\n- **Selecting**: Strand waterfall to find candidate beads\n- **Claiming**: Atomic bead claiming with flock coordination\n- **Building**: Prompt construction from bead\n- **Dispatching**: Rate limiting and adapter resolution\n- **Executing**: Agent process execution with timeout\n- **Handling**: Outcome classification and bead lifecycle management\n- **Logging**: Telemetry and state updates\n- **Exhausted**: All strands returned no work\n- **Stopped**: Graceful shutdown\n- **Errored**: Unrecoverable error\n\n## 2. Bead Selection and Claiming\n\n### Strand Waterfall (`src/strand/mod.rs`)\nThe **StrandRunner** executes a prioritized waterfall:\n1. **Pluck**: Primary bead selection from ready queue\n2. **Mend**: Recovery of stuck/failed beads\n3. **Explore**: Cross-workspace bead discovery\n4. **Weave**: Dependency management\n5. **Unravel**: Stale dependency cleanup\n6. **Pulse**: Periodic task creation\n7. **Reflect**: Learning extraction\n8. **Splice**: Label-based operations\n9. **Knot**: Final verification\n\n**Key features**:\n- Waterfall restarts when strands create new work (cap: 3 restarts)\n- Exclusion filtering prevents re-selecting race-lost beads\n- Each strand emits telemetry for observability\n\n### Claiming System (`src/claim/mod.rs`)\nThe **Claimer** provides atomic bead claiming with coordination:\n- **Per-workspace flock serialization**: Prevents thundering herd on same workspace\n- **Two claiming modes**:\n  - `claim_auto()`: Server-side selection (preferred for multi-worker)\n  - `claim_one()`: Client-side selection with race handling\n- **Race-lost handling**: TTL-based exclusions prevent tight loops\n- **Exclusion sets**: Track recently race-lost beads (30s TTL)\n\n### Ready Queue (`src/bead_store/mod.rs`)\n- **`BeadStore::ready()`**: Returns beads with no incomplete blockers\n- **Server-side claim_auto**: Atomically selects and claims in one transaction\n- **Priority-based ordering**: Lower number = higher priority (P1 > P2 > P3)\n- **Filtering**: Support for assignee and label-based filtering\n\n## 3. Configuration and Telemetry Patterns\n\n### Configuration Hierarchy (`src/config/mod.rs`)\n```\n1. Built-in defaults\n2. Global config (~/.config/needle/config.yaml)\n3. Workspace config (.needle.yaml)\n4. Environment variables (NEEDLE_*)\n5. CLI arguments (highest precedence)\n```\n\n**Key config sections**:\n- **WorkerConfig**: Fleet sizing, timeouts, retry limits\n- **AgentConfig**: Adapter selection, routing rules, timeouts\n- **WorkspaceConfig**: Paths, labels for skill sharing\n- **Strand configs**: Per-strand behavior (pluck, mend, explore, etc.)\n\n### Telemetry System (`src/telemetry/mod.rs`)\n- **Event-driven**: Every state transition emits structured events\n- **JSONL logging**: Durable log files for offline analysis\n- **OpenTelemetry integration**: Span-based tracing with hierarchical relationships\n- **Key events**: `worker.booting`, `worker.started`, `worker.stopped`, `beads.claimed`, `strand.evaluated`\n\n## 4. Coordination and Health Monitoring\n\n### Worker Registry (`src/registry/mod.rs`)\n- **Path**: `~/.needle/state/workers.json`\n- **Flock-protected**: Atomic read-modify-write operations\n- **Dead PID cleanup**: Automatically filters out crashed workers\n- **WorkerEntry structure**: Records worker identity, workspace, beads processed\n\n### Health Monitoring (`src/health/mod.rs`)\n- **Heartbeat files**: `~/.needle/state/heartbeats/{qualified-id}.json`\n- **Background emitter**: Dedicated thread writes heartbeat every N seconds\n- **Shared state**: Main worker updates state via `Arc<Mutex<SharedHeartbeatState>>`\n- **cgov compatibility**: Format matches external capacity governor expectations\n\n**Heartbeat data includes**:\n- Worker state and current bead\n- Workspace and session info\n- Beads processed count\n- Idle status detection\n\n## 5. Supervisor/Relevant Patterns\n\n### Existing Supervisor-like Components\n\n**Mend Strand** (`src/strand/mend.rs`):\n- Detects stuck workers (beads in_progress too long)\n- Cleans up orphaned lock files\n- Deregisters dead workers from registry\n- Runs `br doctor` for database health\n\n**Rate Limiter** (`src/rate_limit/mod.rs`):\n- Per-provider/model rate limiting\n- System resource checks (CPU, memory)\n- Backoff coordination for fleet-wide limits\n\n**Signal Handling** (`src/worker/mod.rs`):\n- Global shutdown flag for graceful termination\n- Unix signal handlers (SIGTERM, SIGINT, SIGHUP)\n- Atexit handler for emergency telemetry\n\n### Watchdog Patterns\n- **HANDLING state watchdog**: 120s timeout with separate thread monitoring\n- **Timeout protection**: Multiple timeout layers (60s inner, 90s outer)\n- **Cancellation tokens**: Arc<AtomicBool> for aborting hung operations\n\n## 6. Key Implementation Insights for Fleet Supervisor\n\n### Worker Launch Coordination\n- **Staggered launches**: Already implemented (2s delay between workers)\n- **TMUX session management**: Each worker isolated in named session\n- **Qualified identity**: `{adapter}-{nato-name}` format for unique identification\n\n### Idle Detection\n- **Exhausted state**: Workers enter this when all strands return no work\n- **IdleAction config**: Controls whether workers wait or exit when idle\n- **Heartbeat idle flag**: Set when worker has no current bead\n\n### Fleet Capacity Limits\n- **max_workers config**: Bounds concurrent workers (default: 4)\n- **Registry-based counting**: Can count live workers via PID liveness\n- **Concurrency enforcement**: Can check registry before launching new workers\n\n### Observability\n- **Comprehensive telemetry**: Every transition emits events\n- **Heartbeat streaming**: Real-time worker state monitoring\n- **Registry queries**: Can list all active workers and their state\n\n### Cross-Workspace Coordination\n- **Explore strand**: Discovers beads from other workspaces\n- **Store switching**: Workers can temporarily switch to remote workspace\n- **Heartbeat workspace updates**: Tracks which workspace worker is processing\n\n## 7. Critical Architecture Points for Supervisor\n\n1. **No central dispatcher**: Workers are independent, self-directed processes\n2. **Shared coordination**: Registry + heartbeat files provide loose coupling\n3. **Graceful degradation**: Workers continue operating if coordination fails\n4. **Idempotent operations**: Registration, claiming, heartbeat are all safe to retry\n5. **Fault tolerance**: Extensive error recovery and timeout protection\n6. **Hot-reload support**: Workers can reload configuration and resume operation\n\nThis architecture is well-suited for a fleet supervisor that:\n- Monitors registry and heartbeats for fleet state\n- Launches workers into workspaces with available beads\n- Implements capacity limits based on worker counts\n- Provides graceful scaling and failure recovery\n- Uses existing telemetry for observability and debugging"}]}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"a89d22d4-b381-4e40-a7a5-355113b4a5c7","uuid":"95d21591-0d0b-4e69-a9ff-4711a37466f1","timestamp":"2026-06-24T12:23:58.510Z","tool_use_result":{"status":"completed","prompt":"Explore the NEEDLE codebase to understand:\n1. Critical Architecture Points for Supervisor\n\n1. **No central dispatcher**: Workers are independent, self-directed processes\n2. **Shared coordination**: Registry + heartbeat files provide loose coupling\n3. **Graceful degradation**: Workers continue operating if coordination fails\n4. **Idempotent operations**: Registration, claiming, heartbeat are all safe to retry\n5. **Fault tolerance**: Extensive error recovery and timeout protection\n6. **Hot-reload support**: Workers can reload configuration and resume operation\n\nThis architecture is well-suited for a fleet supervisor that:\n- Monitors registry and heartbeats for fleet state\n- Launches workers into workspaces with available beads\n- Implements capacity limits based on worker counts\n- Provides graceful scaling and failure recovery\n- Uses existing telemetry for observability and debugging"}],"resolvedModel":"glm-4.7","totalDurationMs":110978,"totalTokens":76880,"totalToolUseCount":27,"usage":{"input_tokens":230,"cache_creation_input_tokens":0,"cache_read_input_tokens":74944,"output_tokens":1706,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[],"speed":"standard"},"toolStats":{"readCount":12,"searchCount":0,"bashCount":15,"editFileCount":0,"linesAdded":0,"linesRemoved":0,"otherToolCount":0}}}

──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n22\t\n23\t/// Check if a process with the given PID is currently running.\n24\t///\n25\t/// Returns `false` if the PID concurrency limits.\n28\tpub fn is_pid_alive(pid: u32) -> bool {\n29\t    #[cfg(unix)]\n30\t    {\n31\t        // Use libc::kill with signal 0 to check if process exists.\n32\t        // SAFETY: kill(pid, 0) only checks existence, doesn't send a signal.\n33\t        unsafe {\n34\t            let ret = libc::kill(pid as i32, 0);\n35\t            if ret == 0 {\n36\t                // kill           // We must read errno immediately after kill, before any other syscalls.\n41\t            let errno = *libc::__errno_location();\n42\t            match errno {\n43\t                libc::ESRCH => {\n44\t                    // No such process: PID is dead.\n45\t                    false\n46\t                }\n47\t                libc::EPERM => {\n48\t                    // }\n52\t                _ => {\n53\t                    // Other errors (e.g., EINVAL for invalid signal).\n54\t                    // Conservatively treat as dead to avoid false positives.\n55\t                    false\n56\t                }\n57\t            }\n58\t        }\n59\t    }\n60\t\n61\t    #[cfg(not(unix))]\n62\t    {\n63\t        // On non-Unix platforms, conservatively return false
