Worker state registry — shared JSON file tracking all active workers.\n//!\n//! The registry is informational: \"who is running, what are they doing, how\n//! many beads processed.\" It is NOT used for coordination — heartbeats handle\n//! that.\n//!\n//! File: `~/.needle/state/workers.json`\n//! Access: flock-protected read-modify-write (atomic updates).\n//!\n//! Depends on: `config`, `types`.\n\nuse std::path::{Path, PathBuf};\n\nuse anyhow::{Context, Result};\nuse chrono::{DateTime, Utc};\nuse fs2::FileExt;\nuse serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// PID liveness checking (platform-specific)\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/// Check if a process with the given PID is currently running.\n///\n/// Returns `false` if the PID does not exist or if we lack permission to signal it.\n/// This is best-effort: if we can't determine liveness, we assume the process is dead\n/// to avoid counting stale entries toward concurrency limits.\npub fn is_pid_alive(pid: u32) -> bool {\n    #[cfg(unix)]\n    {\n        // Use libc::kill with signal 0 to check if process exists.\n        // SAFETY: kill(pid, 0) only checks existence, doesn't send a signal.\n        unsafe {\n            let ret = libc::kill(pid as i32, 0);\n            if ret == 0 {\n                // kill succeeded: process exists and we have permission to signal it.\n                return true;\n            }\n            // kill failed: check errno to distinguish EPERM from ESRCH.\n            // We must read errno immediately after kill, before any other syscalls.\n            let errno = *libc::__errno_location();\n            match errno {\n                libc::ESRCH => {\n                    // No such process: PID is dead.\n                    false\n                }\n                libc::EPERM => {\n                    // Process exists but we don't have permission to signal it.\n                    // We treat this as \"alive\" since the process actually exists.\n                    true\n                }\n                _ => {\n                    // Other errors (e.g., EINVAL for invalid signal).\n                    // Conservatively treat as dead to avoid false positives.\n                    false\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    #[cfg(not(unix))]\n    {\n        // On non-Unix platforms, conservatively return false (assume dead).\n        // This prevents false positives where dead PIDs are counted as live.\n        // TODO: Implement Windows liveness check via OpenProcess if needed.\n        false\n    }\n}\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// WorkerEntry\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/// A single worker entry in the registry.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq)]\npub struct WorkerEntry {\n    /// Fully-qualified worker identity (`{adapter}-{worker_id}`, e.g., `claude-foxtrot`).\n    pub id: String,\n    /// Process ID of the worker.\n    pub pid: u32,\n    /// Workspace the worker is processing beads from.\n    pub workspace: PathBuf,\n    /// Agent adapter name.\n    pub agent: String,\n    /// Model name (if known).\n    pub model: Option<String>,\n    /// Provider name (e.g., `anthropic`, `openai`).\n    pub provider: Option<String>,\n    /// When the worker started.\n    pub started_at: DateTime<Utc>,\n    /// Number of beads processed so far.\n    pub beads_processed: u64,\n}\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// RegistryFile\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/// The on-disk JSON structure for `workers.json`.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub struct RegistryFile {\n    pub workers: Vec<WorkerEntry>,\n    pub updated_at: DateTime<Utc>,\n}\n\nimpl Default for RegistryFile {\n    fn default() -> Self {\n        RegistryFile {\n            workers: Vec::new(),\n            updated_at: Utc::now(),\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Registry\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/// Worker state registry with flock-protected read-modify-write access.\n#[derive(Clone)]\npub struct Registry {\n    path: PathBuf,\n}\n\nimpl Registry {\n    /// Create a registry instance pointing at the given state directory.\n    ///\n    /// The directory will be created on first write if it does not exist.\n    pub fn new(state_dir: &Path) -> Self {\n        Registry {\n            path: state_dir.join(\"workers.json\"),\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Create a registry using the default state directory (`~/.needle/state`).\n    pub fn default_location(needle_home: &Path) -> Self {\n        Self::new(&needle_home.join(\"state\"))\n    }\n\n    /// Path to the workers.json file.\n    pub fn path(&self) -> &Path {\n        &self.path\n    }\n\n    /// Register a worker on startup.\n    ///\n    /// If a worker with the same ID already exists, it is replaced (handles\n    /// stale entries from previous runs).\n    pub fn register(&self, entry: WorkerEntry) -> Result<()> {\n        self.modify(|reg| {\n            // Remove any existing entry with the same ID (stale from previous run).\n            reg.workers.retain(|w| w.id != entry.id);\n            reg.workers.push(entry);\n        })\n    }\n\n    /// Deregister a worker on shutdown.\n    ///\n    /// Best-effort: errors are logged but not propagated to avoid masking\n    /// the real shutdown reason.\n    pub fn deregister(&self, worker_id: &str) -> Result<()> {\n        self.modify(|reg| {\n            reg.workers.retain(|w| w.id != worker_id);\n        })\n    }\n\n    /// Update a worker's beads_processed count.\n    pub fn update_beads_processed(&self, worker_id: &str, beads_processed: u64) -> Result<()> {\n        self.modify(|reg| {\n            if let Some(entry) = reg.workers.iter_mut().find(|w| w.id == worker_id) {\n                entry.beads_processed = beads_processed;\n            }\n        })\n    }\n\n    /// Update a worker's current workspace.\n    pub fn update_workspace(&self, worker_id: &str, workspace: &Path) -> Result<()> {\n        self.modify(|reg| {\n            if let Some(entry) = reg.workers.iter_mut().find(|w| w.id == worker_id) {\n                entry.workspace = workspace.to_path_buf();\n            }\n        })\n    }\n\n    /// Read all registered workers, filtering out entries for dead PIDs.\n    ///\n    /// This lazy cleanup ensures that workers killed via SIGKILL or crashes\n    /// don't accumulate in the registry and falsely count toward concurrency\n    /// limits.\n    pub fn list(&self) -> Result<Vec<WorkerEntry>> {\n        let reg = self.read()?;\n        let total_count = reg.workers.len();\n        let live_workers: Vec<WorkerEntry> = reg\n            .workers\n            .into_iter()\n            .filter(|w| is_pid_alive(w.pid))\n            .collect();\n\n        // If we filtered out dead entries, persist the cleanup so we don't\n        // need to re-check their PIDs on every read. This is best-effort:\n        // if the write fails (disk full, race with another writer), we still\n        // return the correctly filtered list — we'll just re-filter next time.\n        let live_count = live_workers.len();\n        if live_count != total_count {\n            let dead_count = total_count - live_count;\n            tracing::debug!(dead_count, \"filtered dead worker entries from registry\");\n\n            let cleaned_reg = RegistryFile {\n                workers: live_workers.clone(),\n                updated_at: Utc::now(),\n            };\n            if let Err(e) = self.write_cleaned(cleaned_reg) {\n                tracing::warn!(\n                    error = %e,\n                    \"failed to persist dead PID cleanup; will re-filter next read\"\n                );\n            }\n        }\n\n        Ok(live_workers)\n    }\n\n    /// Write a cleaned registry file (used by list() to persist dead PID filtering).\n    ///\n    /// No file locking: read() already released its shared lock before returning.\n    /// The atomic rename ensures concurrent writers don't corrupt the file —\n    /// one write wins cleanly.\n    fn write_cleaned(&self, reg: RegistryFile) -> Result<()> {\n        let tmp_path = self.path.with_extension(\"json.tmp\");\n        let json = serde_json::to_string_pretty(&reg).context(\"failed to serialize registry\")?;\n        std::fs::write(&tmp_path, &json)\n            .with_context(|| format!(\"failed to write temp registry: {}\", tmp_path.display()))?;\n        std::fs::rename(&tmp_path, &self.path).with_context(|| {\n            format!(\"failed to rename temp registry to: {}\", self.path.display())\n        })?;\n        Ok(())\n    }\n\n    // ── Internal helpers ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n    /// Read the registry file, returning a default if it doesn't exist.\n    fn read(&self) -> Result<RegistryFile> {\n        if !self.path.exists() {\n            return Ok(RegistryFile::default());\n        }\n\n        let file = std::fs::File::open(&self.path)\n            .with_context(|| format!(\"failed to open registry: {}\", self.path.display()))?;\n\n        // Shared lock for reading (use fs2 trait method explicitly for MSRV compat).\n        FileExt::lock_shared(&file)\n            .with_context(|| format!(\"failed to acquire shared lock: {}\", self.path.display()))?;\n\n        let content = std::fs::read_to_string(&self.path)\n            .with_context(|| format!(\"failed to read registry: {}\", self.path.display()))?;\n\n        FileExt::unlock(&file)\n            .with_context(|| format!(\"failed to release lock: {}\", self.path.display()))?;\n\n        if content.trim().is_empty() {\n            return Ok(RegistryFile::default());\n        }\n\n        serde_json::from_str(&content)\n            .with_context(|| format!(\"failed to parse registry JSON: {}\", self.path.display()))\n    }\n\n    /// Perform a flock-protected read-modify-write operation.\n    fn modify<F>(&self, mutator: F) -> Result<()>\n    where\n        F: FnOnce(&mut RegistryFile),\n    {\n        // Ensure the parent directory exists.\n        if let Some(parent) = self.path.parent() {\n            std::fs::create_dir_all(parent).with_context(|| {\n                format!(\"failed to create registry directory: {}\", parent.display())\n            })?;\n        }\n\n        // Open or create the file.\n        let file = std::fs::OpenOptions::new()\n            .read(true)\n            .write(true)\n            .create(true)\n            .truncate(false)\n            .open(&self.path)\n            .with_context(|| format!(\"failed to open registry: {}\", self.path.display()))?;\n\n        // Exclusive lock for writing (use fs2 trait method explicitly for MSRV compat).\n        FileExt::lock_exclusive(&file).with_context(|| {\n            format!(\"failed to acquire exclusive lock: {}\", self.path.display())\n        })?;\n\n        // Read current contents.\n        let content = std::fs::read_to_string(&self.path).unwrap_or_default();\n        let mut reg: RegistryFile = if content.trim().is_empty() {\n            RegistryFile::default()\n        } else {\n            serde_json::from_str(&content).unwrap_or_default()\n        };\n\n        // Apply the mutation.\n        mutator(&mut reg);\n        reg.updated_at = Utc::now();\n\n        // Write back atomically: write to temp file, then rename.\n        let tmp_path = self.path.with_extension(\"json.tmp\");\n        let json = serde_json::to_string_pretty(&reg).context(\"failed to serialize registry\")?;\n        std::fs::write(&tmp_path, &json)\n            .with_context(|| format!(\"failed to write temp registry: {}\", tmp_path.display()))?;\n        std::fs::rename(&tmp_path, &self.path).with_context(|| {\n            format!(\"failed to rename temp registry to: {}\", self.path.display())\n        })?;\n\n        // Release lock (use fs2 trait method explicitly for MSRV compat).\n        FileExt::unlock(&file)\n            .with_context(|| format!(\"failed to release lock: {}\", self.path.display()))?;\n\n        Ok(())\n    }\n}\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Tests\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n\n    fn make_entry(id: &str) -> WorkerEntry {\n        WorkerEntry {\n            id: id.to_string(),\n            pid: std::process::id(),\n            workspace:   started_at: Utc::now(),\n            beads_processed: 0,\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// A PID that is definitely not in use on any real system.\n    ///\n    /// This must be a value that:\n    /// 1. Is positive when cast to i32 (avoid -1 special case)\n    /// 2. Is unlikely to ever be assigned by the kernel\n    ///\n    /// We use 9999999 which is well beyond typical PID ranges.\n    const DEAD_PID: u32 = 9999999;\n\n    #[test]\n    fn register_and_list() {\n        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();\n        let reg = Registry::new(dir.path());\n\n        reg.register(make_entry(\"alpha\")).unwrap();\n        reg.register(make_entry(\"bravo\")).unwrap();\n\n        let workers = reg.list().unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(workers.len(), 2);\n        assert_eq!(workers[0].id, \"alpha\");\n        assert_eq!(workers[1].id, \"bravo\");\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn deregister_removes_worker() {\n        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();\n        let reg = Registry::new(dir.path());\n\n    assert_eq!(workers.len(), 1);\n        assert_eq!(workers[0].id, \"bravo\");\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn deregister_nonexistent_is_noop() {\n        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();\n        let reg = Registry::new(dir.path());\n\n        reg.register(make_entry(\"alpha\")).unwrap();\n        reg.deregister(\"nonexistent\").unwrap();\n\n        let workers = reg.list().unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(workers.len(), 1);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn register_replaces_stale_entry() {\n        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();\n        let reg = Registry::new(dir.path());\n\n update_nonexistent_worker_is_noop() {\n        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();\n        let reg = Registry::new(dir.path());\n\n        reg.register(make_entry(\"alpha\")).unwrap();\n        reg.update_beads_processed(\"ghost\", 10).unwrap();\n\n        let workers = reg.list().unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(workers[0].beads_processed, 0);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn assert!(workers.is_empty());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn registry_file_is_valid_json() {\n        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();\n        let reg = Registry::new(dir.path());\n\n   assert_eq!(parsed.workers.len(), 1);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn default_location_uses_state_subdir() {\n        let reg = Registry::default_location(Path::new(\"/home/test/.needle\"));\n        assert_eq!(\n            reg.path(),\n            Path::new(\"/home/test/.needle/state/workers.json\")\n        );\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn concurrent_registration_no_corruption() {\n        let #[test]\n    fn registry_json_roundtrip() {\n        let entry = make_entry(\"alpha\");\n        let reg_file = RegistryFile {\n            workers: vec![entry.clone()],\n            updated_at: assert_eq!(parsed.workers.len(), 1);\n        assert_eq!(parsed.workers[0].id, entry.id);\n        assert_eq!(parsed.workers[0].pid, entry.pid);\n        assert_eq!(parsed.workers[0].agent, entry.agent);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn list_filters_out_dead_pids() {\n        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();\n        let reg = Registry::new(dir.path());\n\n        // Register a live worker (current process).\n        reg.register(make_entry(\"live-alpha\")).unwrap();\n\n        // Create an entry with a fake (dead) PID.\n        let mut dead_entry = make_entry(\"dead-worker\");\n        dead_entry.pid = DEAD_PID;\n\n        // Write the dead entry directly to the file.\n        let file_content = serde_json::to_string_pretty(&RegistryFile {\n            workers: vec![make_entry(\"live-alpha\"), dead_entry],\n            updated_at: Utc::now(),\n        })\n        .unwrap();\n        std::fs::write(reg.path(), file_content).unwrap();\n\n        // list() should filter out the dead PID.\n        let workers = reg.list().unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(workers.len(), 1);\n        assert_eq!(workers[0].id, \"live-alpha\");\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn list_persists_cleanup_after_filtering_dead_pids() {\n        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();\n        let reg = Registry::new(dir.path());\n\n        // Register a live worker.\n ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n398\t// Command handlers\n399\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n400\t\n401\t/// `needle run` — launch a worker.\n402\t///\n403\t/// Always creates dedicated tmux sessions for workers, The only exception is a re-entrant inner\n405\t/// invocation launched by `launch_in_tmux()`, which is identified by the\n406\t/// `NEEDLE_INNER=1` environment variable and runs the worker global.workspace.default.clone()\n422\t    };\n423\t\n424\t    // Load full resolved config (global → workspace .needle.yaml → env → CLI).\n425\t    let cli_overrides = CliOverrides {\n426\t       \"resuming worker after hot-reload (no prior state found)\"\n472\t                );\n473\t            }\n474\t        }\n475\t\n476\t        let tel = crate::telemetry::Telemetry::from_config(worker_id.clone(), &config.telemetry)\n477\t            .unwrap_or_else(|e| {\n478\t                tracing::warn!(error = %e, \"hook telemetry init failed, falling back\");\n479\t                crate::telemetry::Telemetry::new(worker_id.clone())\n480\t            });\n481\t        tel.emit(crate::telemetry::EventKind::UpgradeCompleted {\n482\t launched by launch_in_tmux() — run\n490\t        // the worker directly inside the dedicated session already created.\n491\t        let worker_id = identifier\n492\t            .clone()\n493\t            .unwrap_or_else(|| NATO_ALPHABET[0].to_string());\n494\t        let agent_name = agent.as_deref().unwrap_or(&config.agent.default);\n495\t        let session_name = sanitize_session_name(&format!(\"needle-{agent_name}-{worker_id}\"));\n496\t        tracing::info!(worker = %worker_id, session = %session_name, \"starting worker (inner re-entrant invocation)\");\n497\t        run_worker(config, worker_id)\n498\t    } else {\n499\t        // Always create dedicated tmux sessions, even if already inside tmux.\n500\t        launch_workers(\n501\t            config, workspace, agent, count, identifier, timeout, hot_reload,\n502\t        )\n503\t    }\n504\t}\n505\t\n506\t/// Launch `count` workers in separate tmux sessions with staggered startup delays.\n507\tfn launch_workers(\n508\t    config: Config,\n509\t    workspace: Option<PathBuf>,\n510\t    agent: Option<String>,\n511\t    count: u32,\n512\t    identifier: Option<String>,\n513\t    timeout: Option<u64>,\n514\t    hot_reload: Option<bool>,\n515\t) -> Result<()> {\n516\t    let agent_name = agent\n517\t        .as_deref()\n518\t        .unwrap_or(&config.agent.default)\n519\t        .to_string();\n520\t    let stagger_secs = config.worker.launch_stagger_seconds;\n521\t    let max_workers = config.worker.max_workers;\n522\t\n523\t    if count == 0 {\n524\t        bail!(\"--count must be at least 1\");\n525\t    }\n526\t    if count as usize > NATO_ALPHABET.len() {\n527\t        bail!(\n528\t            \"--count {} exceeds the maximum of {} (NATO alphabet size)\",\n529\t            count,\n530\t            NATO_ALPHABET.len()\n531\t        );\n532\t    }\n533\t\n534\t    // Enforce max_workers cap (0 means max_workers,\n539\t            \"count exceeds max_workers; capping\"\n540\t        );\n541\t        eprintln!(\n542\t            \"Warning: --count {count} exceeds max_workers={max_workers}; launching {max_workers} workers\"\n543\t        );\n544\t        max_workers\n545\t    } else {\n546\t        count\n547\t    };\n548\t\n549\t    // --identifier is only meaningful for a single worker.\n550\t    if effective_count > 1 && identifier.is_some() {\n551\t        bail!(\"--identifier cannot be combined with --count > 1; identifiers are auto-assigned from the NATO alphabet\");\n552\t    }\n553\t\n554\t    // Detect existing sessions to avoid name collisions.\n555\t    let occupied = occupied_worker_ids(&agent_name)?;\n556\t    if !occupied.is_empty() {\n557\t        tracing::info!(\n558\t            occupied = ?occupied,\n559\t            \"found existing worker sessions\"\n560\t        );\n561\t    }\n562\t\n563\t    // Reject --identifier collision early.\n564\t    if let Some(ref id) = identifier {\n565\t        if occupied.contains(id) {\n566\t            bail!(\n567\t                \"worker '{}' is already running in session 'needle-{}-{}'\",\n568\t                id,\n569\t                agent_name,\n570\t                id\n571\t            );\n572\t        }\n573\t    }\n574\t\n575\t    // Build the list of worker IDs, skipping occupied names.\n576\t    let worker_ids: Vec<String> = if effective_count == 1 {\n577\t        vec![identifier.clone().unwrap_or_else(|| {\n578\t            // Pick the first available NATO name.\n579\t            NATO_ALPHABET\n580\t                .iter()\n581\t                .find(|name| !occupied.contains(**name))\n582\t                .map(|s| s.to_string())\n583\t                .unwrap_or_else(|| NATO_ALPHABET[0].to_string())\n584\t        })]\n585\t    } else {\n586\t        let mut ids = Vec::with_capacity(effective_count as usize);\n587\t        for name in NATO_ALPHABET {\n588\t            if ids.len() >= effective_count as usize {\n589\t                break;\n590\t            }\n591\t            if occupied.contains(*name) {\n592\t                tracing::warn!(\n593\t                    worker_id = %name,\n594\t                    \"skipping occupied worker name\"\n595\t                );\n596\t                continue;\n597\t            }\n598\t            ids.push(name.to_string());\n599\t        }\n600\t        if ids.len() < effective_count as usize {\n601\t            bail!(\n602\t                \"cannot launch {} workers — only ids\n609\t    };\n610\t\n611\t    for (seq, worker_id) in worker_ids.iter().enumerate() {\n612\t        let session_name = sanitize_session_name(&format!(\"needle-{agent_name}-{worker_id}\"));\n613\t\n614\t        tracing::info!(\n615\t            worker_id = %worker_id,\n616\t            sequence = seq,\n617\t effective_count,\n640\t            worker_id\n641\t        );\n642\t    }\n643\t\n644\t    let sanitized_agent = sanitize_session_name(&agent_name);\n645\t    if effective_count > 1 {\n646\t needle-{sanitized_agent}-{worker_id}\");\n653\t    }\n654\t\n655\t    Ok(())\n656\t}\n657\t\n658\t/// Initialize the tracing subscriber with OTLP layer if configured.\n659\t///\n660\t/// This must be called before any tracing spans are created so that the OTLP\n661\t/// layer can export them to the configured collector.\n662\t///\n663\t/// Note: Shutdown is handled by the OtlpSink in the telemetry module, not here.\n664\t#[cfg(feature = \"otlp\")]\n665\tfn init_tracing_subscriber(\n666\t    worker_id: String,\n667\t    session_id: String,\n668\t    config: &crate::config::Config,\n669\t) -> Result<()> {\n670\t    use opentelemetry::trace::TracerProvider;\n671\t    use tracing_subscriber::{layer::SubscriberExt, util::SubscriberInitExt};\n672\t\n673\t    let use_ansi = use_ansi();\n674\t\n675\t    // Check if OTLP is enabled\n676\t    if !config.telemetry.otlp_sink.enabled {\n677\t        // No OTLP - just initialize with fmt layer\n678\t        let fmt_layer = .with(fmt_layer)\n683\t            .try_init()\n684\t            .context(\"failed to initialize tracing subscriber\")?;\n685\t        return Ok(());\n686\t    }\n687\t\n688\t    // OTLP is enabled - attributes\n693\t    let resource = crate::telemetry::otlp::OtlpSink::build_resource(\n694\t        &worker_id,\n695\t        &session_id,\n696\t        otlp_config,\n697\t        Some(&config.agent.default),\n698\t        None, // model - not available in AgentConfig\n699\t        config.workspace.default.to_str(),\n700\t    )\n701\t    .context(\"failed to build OTel resource\")?;\n702\t\n703\t    // Create drop channel for tracing layer\n704\t    let (drop_tx, mut drop_rx) =\n705\t        tokio::sync::mpsc::unbounded_channel::<crate::telemetry::otlp::DropEvent>();\n706\t    tokio::spawn(async move {\n707\t        while let Some(drop) = drop_rx.recv().await {\n708\t            tracing::warn!(\n709\t                signal = drop.signal.as_str(),\n710\t                dropped_count = drop.dropped_count,\n711\t                \"OTLP tracing layer export failure\"\n712\t            );\n713\t        }\n714\t    });\n715\t\n716\t    // Build exporters and tracer provider based on protocol\n717\t    let (tracer_provider, ..) = match otlp_config.protocol.as_str() {\n718\t        \"grpc\" => {\n719\t            crate::telemetry::otlp::OtlpSink::build_grpc_providers(otlp_config, &resource, drop_tx)?\n720\t        }\n721\t        \"http\" | \"http/protobuf\" => {\n722\t 'http'\"),\n725\t    };\n726\t\n727\t    // Create the OpenTelemetry tracing layer\n728\t    let otlp_layer = tracing_opentelemetry::layer().with_tracer(tracer_provider.tracer(\"needle\"));\n729\t\n730\t    // Create a fmt layer that works with any subscriber implementing LookupSpan\n731\t    // We build this after the OTLP layer to ensure proper type compatibility\n732\t    let subscriber = tracing_subscriber::registry().with(otlp_layer).with(\n733\t        tracing_subscriber::fmt::layer()\n734\t            .with_writer(std::io::stderr)\n735\t            .with_ansi(use_ansi),\n736\t    );\n737\t\n738\t    subscriber\n739\t        .try_init()\n740\t        .context(\"failed to initialize tracing subscriber with OTLP\")?;\n741\t\n742\t    Ok(())\n743\t}\n744\t\n745\t/// No-op tracing initialization when OTLP feature is disabled.\n746\t#[cfg(not(feature = \"otlp\"))]\n747\tfn state machine (called when inside tmux or for direct mode).\n789\t///\n790\t/// Creates telemetry and the tokio runtime *before* any other initialization\n791\t/// so that `worker.booting` is the very first JSONL event. Each subsequent\n792\t/// init step is wrapped with `init.step.started` / `init.step.completed` so a\n793\t/// silent hang pinpoints the exact blocking call.\n794\tfn run_worker(config: Config, worker_name: String) -> Result<()> {\n795\t    let boot_start = Instant::now();\n796\t    let qualified_id = format!(\"{}-{}\", config.agent.default, worker_name);\n797\t\n798\t    // Phase 0: create tokio runtime + telemetry, emit worker.booting immediately.\n799\t    // Emit eprintln diagnostics before each step so hangs are visible even if telemetry fails.\n800\t    eprintln!(\"NEEDLE worker boot: creating tokio runtime...\");\n801\t    let rt = tokio::runtime::Runtime::new().context(\"failed to create tokio runtime\")?;\n802\t    eprintln!(\"NEEDLE worker boot: tokio runtime created\");\n803\t\n804\t    eprintln!(\"NEEDLE worker boot: initializing tracing subscriber...\");\n805\t    let session_id = generate_session_id_for_worker();\n806\t    init_tracing_subscriber(qualified_id.clone(), session_id.clone(), &config)?;\n807\t    eprintln!(\"NEEDLE worker boot: tracing subscriber initialized\");\n808\t\n809\t    eprintln!(\"NEEDLE worker created\");\n816\t\n817\t    // Emit worker.booting SYNCHRONOUSLY before starting the async writer.\n818\t    // This guarantees the event is written to disk even if start_and_wait() hangs.\n819\t    // Fixes the silent pre-init deadlock where the JSONL file is created but empty.\n820\t    eprintln!(\"NEEDLE worker boot: emitting worker.booting event (sync)...\");\n821\t    telemetry.emit_sync(EventKind::WorkerBooting {\n822\t        worker_name: worker_name.clone(),\n823\t        version: env!(\"CARGO_PKG_VERSION\").to_string(),\n824\t    })?;\n825\t    eprintln!(\"NEEDLE worker boot: worker.booting written to disk\");\n826\t\n827\t    // Start the async writer thread after worker.booting is on disk.\n828\t    eprintln!(\"NEEDLE worker boot: starting telemetry writer thread...\");\n829\t    rt.block_on(telemetry.start_and_wait())\n830\t        .context(\"writer thread failed to start\")?;\n831\t    eprintln!(\"NEEDLE worker boot: writer thread Ok(Worker::new_with_telemetry(\n849\t            config,\n850\t            worker_name.clone(),\n851\t            store,\n852\t            telemetry.clone(),\n853\t        ))\n854\t    })?;\n855\t\n856\t    // Boot timeout guard: self-abort if init took >60 s.\n857\t    let elapsed_ms = boot_start.elapsed().as_millis() as u64;\n858\t    if elapsed_ms > 60_000 {\n859\t        telemetry.emit(EventKind::WorkerBootTimeout { elapsed_ms })?;\n860\t        bail!(\"boot exceeded 60 s ({elapsed_ms} ms), aborting\");\n861\t    }\n862\t\n863\t    eprintln!(\n864\t        \"NEEDLE worker boot: all init steps completed in {elapsed_ms}ms, starting worker loop...\"\n865\t    );\n866\t    let result = rt.block_on(worker.run())?;\n867\t\n868\t    tracing::info!(final_state = %result, \"worker finished\");\n869\t    Ok(())\n870\t}\n871\t\n872\t/// Emit start/complete telemetry around a fallible initialization step.\n873\t///\n874\t/// Each step's completion is force-flushed to disk so that if a subsequent\n875\t/// step blocks indefinitely, the telemetry log shows exactly where it stopped.\n876\t/// Also emits eprintln diagnostics for visibility even if telemetry hangs.\n877\tfn init_step<T, F>(name: &str, tel: &Telemetry, f: F) -> Result<T>\n878\twhere\n879\t    F: FnOnce() -> Result<T>,\n880\t{\n881\t    eprintln!(\"NEEDLE worker boot: starting tel.force_flush(std::time::Duration::from_secs(1))?;\n895\t    result\n896\t}\n897\t\n898\t/// Returns true when this process is a re-entrant inner invocation launched\n899\t/// by `launch_in_tmux()`, Option<String>,\n910\t    timeout: Option<u64>,\n911\t    hot_reload: Option<bool>,\n912\t) -> Result<()> {\n913\t    // Build the command that tmux will run inside the session.\n914\t    let self_exe        inner_args.push(id.clone());\n931\t    }\n932\t    if let Some(t) = timeout {\n933\t        inner_args.push(\"--timeout\".to_string());\n934\t        inner_args.push(t.to_string());\n935\t ValueEnum)]\npub enum ListFormat {\n    Table,\n    Json,\n}\n\n/// Output format for the logs command.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, ValueEnum)]\npub enum LogFormat {\n    /// Human-readable table format (default).\n    Table,\n    /// JSON Lines format (one JSON object per line).\n    Json,\n    /// Alias for table (human-readable).\n    Human,\n    /// Alias for json (JSON Lines).\n    Jsonl,\n}\n\n/// Grouping dimension for the `needle stats` command.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, ValueEnum)]\npub enum StatsBy {\n    /// Group by template version tag (e.g., `\"pluck-v2\"`).\n    #[value(name = \"template_version\")]\n    TemplateVersion,\n    /// Group by template name / task type (e.g., `\"pluck\"`).\n    #[value(name = \"task_type\")]\n    TaskType,\n    /// Group by worker identifier (e.g., `\"needle-alpha\"`).\n    #[value(name = \"worker\")]\n    Worker,\n}\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Entry point\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/// Entry point called from `main`.\npub fn run() -> Result<()> {\n    let cli = Cli::parse();\n\n    match cli.command {\n        CliCommand::Run {\n            workspace,\n            agent,\n            count,\n            identifier,\n            timeout,\n            resume,\n            hot_reload,\n        } => cmd_run(\n            workspace, agent, count, identifier, timeout, resume, hot_reload,\n        ),\n        CliCommand::Stop { all, identifier } => cmd_stop(all, identifier),\n        CliCommand::Cleanup { all, identifier } => cmd_cleanup(all, identifier),\n        CliCommand::List { format } => cmd_list(format),\n        CliCommand::Attach { identifier } => cmd_attach(&identifier),\n        CliCommand::Status {\n            format,\n            by_worker,\n            cost,\n            since,\n            until,\n            idle_strands,\n        } => cmd_status(format, by_worker, cost, since, until, idle_strands),\n        CliCommand::Logs {\n            follow,\n            filter,\n            since,\n            until,\n            format,\n        } => cmd_logs(follow, filter, since, until, format),\n        CliCommand::ConfigCmd {\n            get,\n            dump,\n            show_source,\n        } => cmd_config(get, dump, show_source),\n        CliCommand::Doctor { repair, workspace } => cmd_doctor(repair, workspace),\n        CliCommand::Init => cmd_init(),\n        CliCommand::Version => {\n            cmd_version();\n            Ok(())\n        }\n        CliCommand::TestAgent { name } => cmd_test_agent(&name),\n        CliCommand::Canary { status } => cmd_canary(status),\n        CliCommand::Upgrade { check } => cmd_upgrade(check),\n        CliCommand::Rollback => cmd_rollback(),\n        CliCommand::Reflect { workspace, force } => cmd_reflect(workspace, force),\n        CliCommand::UpdateRules { output } => cmd_update_rules(output),\n        CliCommand::Stats {\n            by,\n            since,\n            until,\n            format,\n        } => cmd_stats(by, since, until, format),\n    }\n}\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Command handlers\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/// `needle run` — launch a worker.\n///\n/// Always creates dedicated tmux sessions for workers, even when invoked from\n/// inside an existing tmux session. The only exception is a re-entrant inner\n/// invocation launched by `launch_in_tmux()`, which is identified by the\n/// `NEEDLE_INNER=1` environment variable and runs the worker directly.\nfn cmd_run(\n    workspace: Option<PathBuf>,\n    agent: Option<String>,\n    count: u32,\n    identifier: Option<String>,\n    timeout: Option<u64>,\n    resume: bool,\n    hot_reload: Option<bool>,\n) -> Result<()> {\n    // Determine workspace root (CLI arg → canonicalized, else global default).\n    let workspace_root = if let Some(ref ws) = workspace {\n        ws.canonicalize().unwrap_or_else(|_| ws.clone())\n    } else {\n        let global = ConfigLoader::load_global()?;\n        global.workspace.default.clone()\n    };\n\n    // Load full resolved config (global → workspace .needle.yaml → env → CLI).\n    let cli_overrides = CliOverrides {\n        workspace: Some(workspace_root.clone()),\n        agent_binary: agent.clone(),\n        max_workers: None,\n        ..Default::default()\n    };\n    let (mut config, _) = ConfigLoader::load_resolved(&workspace_root, cli_overrides)?;\n\n    if let Some(t) = timeout {\n        config.agent.timeout = t;\n    }\n\n    if let Some(hr) = hot_reload {\n        config.self_modification.hot_reload = hr;\n    }\n\n    if resume {\n        // Hot-reload resume: inherit worker identity from --identifier,\n        // load state from heartbeat file + registry, continue from SELECTING.\n        let worker_id = identifier\n            .clone()\n            .unwrap_or_else(|| NATO_ALPHABET[0].to_string());\n\n        // Load resume state from heartbeat and registry.\n        let resume_state = crate::upgrade::ResumeState::load(&config, &worker_id)?;\n\n        // Emit upgrade.completed telemetry.\n        let current_hash = crate::upgrade::file_hash(\n            &std::env::current_exe().context(\"failed to locate own binary\")?,\n        )\n        .unwrap_or_else(|_| \"unknown\".to_string());\n\n        match &resume_state {\n            Some(state) => {\n                tracing::info!(\n                    worker = %worker_id,\n                    binary_hash = %&current_hash[..current_hash.len().min(12)],\n                    beads_processed = state.beads_processed,\n                    session = %state.session,\n                    \"resuming worker after hot-reload\"\n                );\n            }\n            None => {\n                tracing::info!(\n                    worker = %worker_id,\n                    binary_hash = %&current_hash[..current_hash.len().min(12)],\n                    \"resuming worker after hot-reload (no prior state found)\"\n                );\n            }\n        }\n\n        let tel = crate::telemetry::Telemetry::from_config(worker_id.clone(), &config.telemetry)\n            .unwrap_or_else(|e| {\n                tracing::warn!(error = %e, \"hook telemetry init failed, falling back\");\n                crate::telemetry::Telemetry::new(worker_id.clone())\n            });\n        tel.emit(crate::telemetry::EventKind::UpgradeCompleted {\n            new_hash: current_hash,\n        })?;\n\n        return run_worker(config, worker_id);\n    }\n\n    if is_needle_inner() {\n        // Re-entrant inner invocation launched by launch_in_tmux() — run\n        // the worker directly inside the dedicated session already created.\n        let worker_id = identifier\n            .clone()\n            .unwrap_or_else(|| NATO_ALPHABET[0].to_string());\n        let agent_name = agent.as_deref().unwrap_or(&config.agent.default);\n        let session_name = sanitize_session_name(&format!(\"needle-{agent_name}-{worker_id}\"));\n        tracing::info!(worker = %worker_id, session = %session_name, \"starting worker (inner re-entrant invocation)\");\n        run_worker(config, worker_id)\n    } else {\n        // Always create dedicated tmux sessions, even if already inside tmux.\n        launch_workers(\n            config, workspace, agent, count, identifier, timeout, hot_reload,\n        )\n    }\n}\n\n/// Launch `count` workers in separate tmux sessions with staggered startup delays.\nfn launch_workers(\n    config: Config,\n    workspace: Option<PathBuf>,\n    agent: Option<String>,\n    count: u32,\n    identifier: Option<String>,\n    timeout: Option<u64>,\n    hot_reload: Option<bool>,\n) -> Result<()> {\n    let agent_name = agent\n        .as_deref()\n        .unwrap_or(&config.agent.default)\n        .to_string();\n    let stagger_secs = config.worker.launch_stagger_seconds;\n    let max_workers = config.worker.max_workers;\n\n    if count == 0 {\n        bail!(\"--count must be at least 1\");\n    }\n    if count as usize > NATO_ALPHABET.len() {\n        bail!(\n            \"--count {} exceeds the maximum of {} (NATO alphabet size)\",\n            count,\n            NATO_ALPHABET.len()\n        );\n    }\n\n    // Enforce max_workers cap (0 means unlimited).\n    let effective_count = if max_workers > 0 && count > max_workers {\n        tracing::warn!(\n            requested = count,\n            capped_to = max_workers,\n            \"count exceeds max_workers; capping\"\n        );\n        eprintln!(\n            \"Warning: --count {count} exceeds max_workers={max_workers}; launching {max_workers} workers\"\n        );\n        max_workers\n    } else {\n        count\n    };\n\n    // --identifier is only meaningful for a single worker.\n    if effective_count > 1 && identifier.is_some() {\n        bail!(\"--identifier cannot be combined with --count > 1; identifiers are auto-assigned from the NATO alphabet\");\n    }\n\n    // Detect existing sessions to avoid name collisions.\n    let occupied = occupied_worker_ids(&agent_name)?;\n    if !occupied.is_empty() {\n        tracing::info!(\n            occupied = ?occupied,\n            \"found existing worker sessions\"\n        );\n    }\n\n    // Reject --identifier collision early.\n    if let Some(ref id) = identifier {\n        if occupied.contains(id) {\n            bail!(\n                \"worker '{}' is already running in session 'needle-{}-{}'\",\n                id,\n                agent_name,\n                id\n            );\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Build the list of worker IDs, skipping occupied names.\n    let worker_ids: Vec<String> = if effective_count == 1 {\n        vec![identifier.clone().unwrap_or_else(|| {\n            // Pick the first available NATO name.\n            NATO_ALPHABET\n                .iter()\n                .find(|name| !occupied.contains(**name))\n                .map(|s| s.to_string())\n                .unwrap_or_else(|| NATO_ALPHABET[0].to_string())\n        })]\n    } else {\n        let mut ids = Vec::with_capacity(effective_count as usize);\n        for name in NATO_ALPHABET {\n            if ids.len() >= effective_count as usize {\n                break;\n            }\n            if occupied.contains(*name) {\n                tracing::warn!(\n                    worker_id = %name,\n                    \"skipping occupied worker name\"\n                );\n                continue;\n            }\n            ids.push(name.to_string());\n        }\n        if ids.len() < effective_count as usize {\n            bail!(\n                \"cannot launch {} workers — only {} NATO names available ({} occupied)\",\n                effective_count,\n                ids.len(),\n                occupied.len()\n            );\n        }\n        ids\n    };\n\n    for (seq, worker_id) in worker_ids.iter().enumerate() {\n        let session_name = sanitize_session_name(&format!(\"needle-{agent_name}-{worker_id}\"));\n\n        tracing::info!(\n            worker_id = %worker_id,\n            sequence = seq,\n            total = effective_count,\n            session = %session_name,\n            \"launching worker\"\n        );\n\n        // Stagger: sleep before launching subsequent workers.\n        if seq > 0 && stagger_secs > 0 {\n            std::thread::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_secs(stagger_secs));\n        }\n\n        launch_in_tmux(\n            &session_name,\n            workspace.clone(),\n            agent.clone(),\n            Some(worker_id.clone()),\n            timeout,\n            hot_reload,\n        )?;\n\n        println!(\n            \"[{}/{}] Started worker '{}' in tmux session: {session_name}\",\n            seq + 1,\n            effective_count,\n            worker_id\n        );\n    }\n\n    let sanitized_agent = sanitize_session_name(&agent_name);\n    if effective_count > 1 {\n        println!(\n            \"\\nStarted {effective_count} workers (stagger: {stagger_secs}s between launches).\"\n        );\n        println!(\"Attach to a worker with: tmux attach -t needle-{sanitized_agent}-<name>\");\n    } else {\n        let worker_id = &worker_ids[0];\n        println!(\"Attach with: tmux attach -t needle-{sanitized_agent}-{worker_id}\");\n    }\n\n    Ok(())\n}\n\n/// Initialize the tracing subscriber with OTLP layer if configured.\n///\n/// This must be called before any tracing spans are created so that the OTLP\n/// layer can export them to the configured collector.\n///\n/// Note: Shutdown is handled by the OtlpSink in the telemetry module, not here.\n#[cfg(feature = \"otlp\")]\nfn init_tracing_subscriber(\n    worker_id: String,\n    session_id: String,\n    config: &crate::config::Config,\n) -> Result<()> {\n    use opentelemetry::trace::TracerProvider;\n    use tracing_subscriber::{layer::SubscriberExt, util::SubscriberInitExt};\n\n    let use_ansi = use_ansi();\n\n    // Check if OTLP is enabled\n    if !config.telemetry.otlp_sink.enabled {\n        // No OTLP - just initialize with fmt layer\n        let fmt_layer = tracing_subscriber::fmt::layer()\n            .with_writer(std::io::stderr)\n            .with_ansi(use_ansi);\n        tracing_subscriber::registry()\n            .with(fmt_layer)\n            .try_init()\n            .context(\"failed to initialize tracing subscriber\")?;\n        return Ok(());\n    }\n\n    // OTLP is enabled - create the OTLP layer inline\n    // We create it inline to avoid type erasure issues with boxed layers\n    let otlp_config = &config.telemetry.otlp_sink;\n\n    // Build resource attributes\n    let resource = crate::telemetry::otlp::OtlpSink::build_resource(\n        &worker_id,\n        &session_id,\n        otlp_config,\n        Some(&config.agent.default),\n        None, // model - not available in AgentConfig\n        config.workspace.default.to_str(),\n    )\n    .context(\"failed to build OTel resource\")?;\n\n    // Create drop channel for tracing layer\n    let (drop_tx, mut drop_rx) =\n        tokio::sync::mpsc::unbounded_channel::<crate::telemetry::otlp::DropEvent>();\n    tokio::spawn(async move {\n        while let Some(drop) = drop_rx.recv().await {\n            tracing::warn!(\n                signal = drop.signal.as_str(),\n                dropped_count = drop.dropped_count,\n                \"OTLP tracing layer export failure\"\n            );\n        }\n    });\n\n    // Build exporters and tracer provider based on protocol\n    let (tracer_provider, ..) = match otlp_config.protocol.as_str() {\n        \"grpc\" => {\n            crate::telemetry::otlp::OtlpSink::build_grpc_providers(otlp_config, &resource, drop_tx)?\n        }\n        \"http\" | \"http/protobuf\" => {\n            crate::telemetry::otlp::OtlpSink::build_http_providers(otlp_config, &resource, drop_tx)?\n        }\n        other => anyhow::bail!(\"invalid OTLP protocol: {other}, must be 'grpc' or 'http'\"),\n    };\n\n    // Create the OpenTelemetry tracing layer\n    let otlp_layer = tracing_opentelemetry::layer().with_tracer(tracer_provider.tracer(\"needle\"));\n\n    // Create a fmt layer that works with any subscriber implementing LookupSpan\n    // We build this after the OTLP layer to ensure proper type compatibility\n    let subscriber = tracing_subscriber::registry().with(otlp_layer).with(\n        tracing_subscriber::fmt::layer()\n            .with_writer(std::io::stderr)\n            .with_ansi(use_ansi),\n    );\n\n    subscriber\n        .try_init()\n        .context(\"failed to initialize tracing subscriber with OTLP\")?;\n\n    Ok(())\n}\n\n/// No-op tracing initialization when OTLP feature is disabled.\n#[cfg(not(feature = \"otlp\"))]\nfn init_tracing_subscriber(\n    _worker_id: String,\n    _session_id: String,\n    _config: &crate::config::Config,\n) -> Result<()> {\n    use tracing_subscriber::{layer::SubscriberExt, util::SubscriberInitExt};\n\n    let use_ansi = use_ansi();\n\n    // Initialize with just the stdout layer when OTLP is disabled\n    tracing_subscriber::registry()\n        .with(\n            tracing_subscriber::fmt::layer()\n                .with_writer(std::io::stderr)\n                .with_ansi(use_ansi),\n        )\n        .try_init()\n        .context(\"failed to initialize tracing subscriber\")?;\n\n    Ok(())\n}\n\n/// Helper function to determine ANSI support (non-Android platforms).\n#[cfg(not(target_os = \"android\"))]\nfn use_ansi() -> bool {\n    atty::is(atty::Stream::Stderr)\n}\n\n/// Helper function for Android (no ANSI).\n#[cfg(target_os = \"android\")]\nfn use_ansi() -> bool {\n    false\n}\n\n/// Generate a session ID for a worker.\n///\n/// Uses the telemetry module's session ID generator.\nfn generate_session_id_for_worker() -> String {\n    crate::telemetry::generate_session_id()\n}\n\n/// Start the worker state machine (called when inside tmux or for direct mode).\n///\n/// Creates telemetry and the tokio runtime *before* any other initialization\n/// so that `worker.booting` is the very first JSONL event. Each subsequent\n/// init step is wrapped with `init.step.started` / `init.step.completed` so a\n/// silent hang pinpoints the exact blocking call.\nfn run_worker(config: Config, worker_name: String) -> Result<()> {\n    let boot_start = Instant::now();\n    let qualified_id = format!(\"{}-{}\", config.agent.default, worker_name);\n\n    // Phase 0: create tokio runtime + telemetry, emit worker.booting immediately.\n    // Emit eprintln diagnostics before each step so hangs are visible even if telemetry fails.\n    eprintln!(\"NEEDLE worker boot: creating tokio runtime...\");\n    let rt = tokio::runtime::Runtime::new().context(\"failed to create tokio runtime\")?;\n    eprintln!(\"NEEDLE worker boot: tokio runtime created\");\n\n    eprintln!(\"NEEDLE worker boot: initializing tracing subscriber...\");\n    let session_id = generate_session_id_for_worker();\n    init_tracing_subscriber(qualified_id.clone(), session_id.clone(), &config)?;\n    eprintln!(\"NEEDLE worker boot: tracing subscriber initialized\");\n\n    eprintln!(\"NEEDLE worker boot: creating telemetry...\");\n    let telemetry =\n        Telemetry::from_config(qualified_id.clone(), &config.telemetry).unwrap_or_else(|e| {\n            tracing::warn!(error = %e, \"failed to create hook-enabled telemetry, falling back\");\n            Telemetry::new(qualified_id.clone())\n        });\n    eprintln!(\"NEEDLE worker boot: telemetry created\");\n\n    // Emit worker.booting SYNCHRONOUSLY before starting the async writer.\n    // This guarantees the event is written to disk even if start_and_wait() hangs.\n    // Fixes the silent pre-init deadlock where the JSONL file is created but empty.\n    eprintln!(\"NEEDLE worker boot: emitting worker.booting event (sync)...\");\n    telemetry.emit_sync(EventKind::WorkerBooting {\n        worker_name: worker_name.clone(),\n        version: env!(\"CARGO_PKG_VERSION\").to_string(),\n    })?;\n    eprintln!(\"NEEDLE worker boot: worker.booting written to disk\");\n\n    // Start the async writer thread after worker.booting is on disk.\n    eprintln!(\"NEEDLE worker boot: starting telemetry writer thread...\");\n    rt.block_on(telemetry.start_and_wait())\n        .context(\"writer thread failed to start\")?;\n    eprintln!(\"NEEDLE worker boot: writer thread started\");\n\n    // Phase 1: bead store discovery.\n    // Use BfCliBeadStore for atomic server-selected bead claiming.\n    let store: Arc<dyn crate::bead_store::BeadStore> =\n        Arc::new(init_step(\"bead_store_discover\", &telemetry, || {\n            crate::bead_store::BfCliBeadStore::discover(\n                config.workspace.default.clone(),\n                None,                       // model: do not filter by model — beads are untagged\n                Some(\"needle\".to_string()), // harness\n                Some(env!(\"CARGO_PKG_VERSION\").to_string()), // harness_version\n            )\n            .context(\"failed to locate bf CLI for bead store\")\n        })?);\n\n    // Phase 2: worker construction (heavy — prompt loading, adapter discovery, etc.).\n    let mut worker = init_step(\"worker_construction\", &telemetry, || {\n        Ok(Worker::new_with_telemetry(\n            config,\n            worker_name.clone(),\n            store,\n            telemetry.clone(),\n        ))\n    })?;\n\n    // Boot timeout guard: self-abort if init took >60 s.\n    let elapsed_ms = boot_start.elapsed().as_millis() as u64;\n    if elapsed_ms > 60_000 {\n        telemetry.emit(EventKind::WorkerBootTimeout { elapsed_ms })?;\n        bail!(\"boot exceeded 60 s ({elapsed_ms} ms), aborting\");\n    }\n\n    eprintln!(\n        \"NEEDLE worker boot: all init steps completed in {elapsed_ms}ms, starting worker loop...\"\n    );\n    let result = rt.block_on(worker.run())?;\n\n    tracing::info!(final_state = %result, \"worker finished\");\n    Ok(())\n}\n\n/// Emit start/complete telemetry around a fallible initialization step.\n///\n/// Each step's completion is force-flushed to disk so that if a subsequent\n/// step blocks indefinitely, the telemetry log shows exactly where it stopped.\n/// Also emits eprintln diagnostics for visibility even if telemetry hangs.\nfn init_step<T, F>(name: &str, tel: &Telemetry, f: F) -> Result<T>\nwhere\n    F: FnOnce() -> Result<T>,\n{\n    eprintln!(\"NEEDLE worker boot: starting init step '{name}'...\");\n    tel.emit(EventKind::InitStepStarted {\n        step: name.to_string(),\n    })?;\n    let t = Instant::now();\n    let result = f();\n    let elapsed = t.elapsed().as_millis() as u64;\n    tel.emit(EventKind::InitStepCompleted {\n        step: name.to_string(),\n        duration_ms: elapsed,\n    })?;\n    eprintln!(\"NEEDLE worker boot: init step '{name}' completed in {elapsed}ms\");\n    // Force-flush so the step completion is visible before the next (potentially blocking) step.\n    tel.force_flush(std::time::Duration::from_secs(1))?;\n    result\n}\n\n/// Returns true when this process is a re-entrant inner invocation launched\n/// by `launch_in_tmux()`, indicated by `NEEDLE_INNER=1` in the environment.\nfn is_needle_inner() -> bool {\n    std::env::var(\"NEEDLE_INNER\").is_ok_and(|v| v == \"1\")\n}\n\n/// Create a single tmux session and re-exec self inside it with `--count 1`.\nfn launch_in_tmux(\n    session_name: &str,\n    workspace: Option<PathBuf>,\n    agent: Option<String>,\n    identifier: Option<String>,\n    timeout: Option<u64>,\n    hot_reload: Option<bool>,\n) -> Result<()> {\n    // Build the command that tmux will run inside the session.\n    let self_exe = std::env::current_exe().context(\"failed to locate own binary\")?;\n    let mut inner_args = vec![\"run\".to_string()];\n\n    if let Some(ref ws) = workspace {\n        inner_args.push(\"--workspace\".to_string());\n        inner_args.push(ws.display().to_string());\n    }\n    if let Some(ref a) = agent {\n        inner_args.push(\"--agent\".to_string());\n        inner_args.push(a.clone());\n    }\n    // Each session runs exactly one worker; identifier is always resolved before call.\n    inner_args.push(\"--count\".to_string());\n    inner_args.push(\"1\".to_string());\n    if let Some(ref id) = identifier {\n        inner_args.push(\"--identifier\".to_string());\n        inner_args.push(id.clone());\n    }\n    if let Some(t) = timeout {\n        inner_args.push(\"--timeout\".to_string());\n        inner_args.push(t.to_string());\n    }\n    if let Some(hr) = hot_reload {\n        inner_args.push(\"--hot-reload\".to_string());\n        inner_args.push(hr.to_string());\n    }\n\n    // Build stderr log path: ~/.needle/logs/<session_name>.stderr.log\n    let home = std::env::var(\"HOME\").unwrap_or_else(|_| \".\".to_string());\n    let stderr_log = format!(\"{home}/.needle/logs/{session_name}.stderr.log\");\n\n    let inner_cmd = format!(\n        \"NEEDLE_INNER=1 {} {} 2>> {}\",\n        shell_escape(&self_exe.display().to_string()),\n        inner_args\n            .iter()\n            .map(|a| shell_escape(a))\n            .collect::<Vec<_>>()\n            .join(\" \"),\n        shell_escape(&stderr_log)\n    );\n\n    let status = ProcessCommand::new(\"tmux\")\n        .args([\"new-session\", \"-d\", \"-s\", session_name, &inner_cmd])\n        .status()\n        .context(\"failed to launch tmux — is tmux installed?\")?;\n\n    if !status.success() {\n        bail!(\n            \"tmux new-session exited with status {} for session '{}'\",\n            status,\n            session_name\n        );\n    }\n\n    Ok(())\n}\n\n/// `needle stop` — send SIGTERM to worker processes in tmux sessions.\nfn cmd_stop(all: bool, identifier: Option<String>) -> Result<()> {\n    if !all && identifier.is_none() {\n        bail!(\"specify --all or --identifier <NAME>\");\n    }\n\n    let sessions = list_needle_sessions()?;\n\n    if sessions.is_empty() {\n        println!(\"No needle sessions running.\");\n        return Ok(());\n    }\n\n    let targets: Vec<&str> = if all {\n        sessions.iter().map(|s| s.name.as_str()).collect()\n    } else {\n        let id = identifier.as_deref().unwrap_or(\"\");\n        sessions\n            .iter()\n            .filter(|s| s.name.contains(id))\n            .map(|s| s.name.as_str())\n            .collect()\n    };\n\n    if targets.is_empty() {\n        println!(\"No matching sessions found.\");\n        return Ok(());\n    }\n\n    for session in &targets {\n        tracing::info!(session = %session, \"sending SIGTERM to tmux session\");\n        // tmux send-keys sends C-c to the foreground process\n        let status = ProcessCommand::new(\"tmux\")\n            .args([\"send-keys\", \"-t\", session, \"C-c\", \"\"])\n            .status()\n            .with_context(|| format!(\"failed to send SIGTERM to session '{session}'\"))?;\n\n        if status.success() {\n            // Wait up to 5 seconds for the worker to exit gracefully\n            for _ in 0..50 {\n                std::thread::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_millis(100));\n                let session_exists = ProcessCommand::new(\"tmux\")\n                    .args([\"has-session\", \"-t\", session])\n                    .output()\n                    .map(|o| o.status.success())\n                    .unwrap_or(false);\n                if !session_exists {\n                    break;\n                }\n            }\n            // Kill the session to clean up (worker may have already exited)\n            let kill_status = ProcessCommand::new(\"tmux\")\n                .args([\"kill-session\", \"-t\", session])\n                .status();\n            match kill_status {\n                Ok(s) if s.success() => println!(\"Stopped: {session}\"),\n                Ok(_) => println!(\"Stopped: {session} (session already gone)\"),\n                Err(e) => println!(\"Warning: stopped {session} but failed to kill session: {e}\"),\n            }\n        } else {\n            println!(\"Warning: could not stop session '{session}' (status: {status})\");\n        }\n    }\n\n    Ok(())\n}\n\n/// `needle cleanup` — remove orphaned tmux sessions.\n///\n/// Finds and removes needle tmux sessions that no longer have active workers.\n/// With --all, removes all needle sessions regardless of worker status.\nfn cmd_cleanup(all: bool, identifier: Option<String>) -> Result<()> {\n    let sessions = list_needle_sessions()?;\n\n    if sessions.is_empty() {\n        println!(\"No needle sessions running.\");\n        return Ok(());\n    }\n\n    let targets: Vec<&str> = if all {\n        sessions.iter().map(|s| s.name.as_str()).collect()\n    } else {\n        let id = identifier.as_deref().unwrap_or(\"\");\n        sessions\n            .iter()\n            .filter(|s| id.is_empty() || s.name.contains(id))\n            .map(|s| s.name.as_str())\n            .collect()\n    };\n\n    if targets.is_empty() {\n        println!(\"No matching sessions found.\");\n        return Ok(());\n    }\n\n    let mut cleaned = 0;\n    for session in &targets {\n        // Kill the session\n        let status = ProcessCommand::new(\"tmux\")\n            .args([\"kill-session\", \"-t\", session])\n            .status();\n\n        match status {\n            Ok(s) if s.success() => {\n                println!(\"Cleaned up: {session}\");\n                cleaned += 1;\n            }\n            Ok(_) => {\n                println!(\"Warning: session '{session}' already gone\");\n            }\n            Err(e) => {\n                println!(\"Warning: failed to cleanup session '{session}': {e}\");\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    if cleaned == 0 {\n        println!(\"No sessions cleaned up.\");\n    } else {\n        println!(\"Cleaned up {cleaned} session(s).\");\n    }\n\n    Ok(())\n}\n\n/// `needle list` — show running needle sessions.\nfn cmd_list(format: ListFormat) -> Result<()> {\n    let sessions = list_needle_sessions()?;\n\n    if sessions.is_empty() {\n        match format {\n            ListFormat::Table => println!(\"No needle sessions running.\"),\n            ListFormat::Json => println!(\"[]\"),\n        }\n        return Ok(());\n    }\n\n    match format {\n        ListFormat::Table => {\n            println!(\"{:<40} {:<20} {:<10}\", \"SESSION\", \"CREATED\", \"STATUS\");\n            println!(\"{}\", \"-\".repeat(70));\n            for s in &sessions {\n                println!(\"{:<40} {:<20} {:<10}\", s.name, s.created, s.status);\n            }\n        }\n        ListFormat::Json => {\n            let json = serde_json::to_string_pretty(&sessions)\n                .context(\"failed to serialize sessions to JSON\")?;\n            println!(\"{json}\");\n        }\n    }\n\n    Ok(())\n}\n\n/// `needle init` — initialize v2 config with optional v1 migration.\n///\n/// Creates ~/.config/needle/config.yaml. Detects existing v1 artifacts\n/// in ~/.needle/ and migrates compatible settings (agent name, workspace\n/// path, worker count) to the v2 YAML schema. Safe to run on already-\n/// initialized installs (idempotent).\nfn cmd_init() -> Result<()> {\n    /// Resolve a path relative to the user's home directory.\n    fn dirs_or_home(relative: &str) -> PathBuf {\n        if let Some(home) = std::env::var_os(\"HOME\") {\n            PathBuf::from(home).join(relative)\n        } else {\n            PathBuf::from(\"/tmp\").join(relative)\n        }\n    }\n\n    let config_path = dirs_or_home(\".config/needle/config.yaml\");\n    let v1_dir = dirs_or_home(\".needle\");\n\n    // Check if v2 config already exists.\n    if config_path.exists() {\n        println!(\"Config already exists: {}\", config_path.display());\n        let config = ConfigLoader::load_from_path(&config_path)?;\n        println!(\"  Agent default: {}\", config.agent.default);\n        println!(\"  Workspace: {}\", config.workspace.default.display());\n        println!(\"  Max workers: {}\", config.worker.max_workers);\n        println!(\"\\nTo reinitialize, delete the existing config file first.\");\n        return Ok(());\n    }\n\n    // Detect v1 artifacts and migrate compatible settings.\n    let mut agent_name = None::<String>;\n    let mut workspace_path = None::<PathBuf>;\n\n    // Check for v1 directory.\n    if v1_dir
